# 第 17 章　英雄未死之前

*3.5 版本主视觉　·　图源：米游社*

> Before Their Deaths　·　英雄未死之前

## 142　Time, Ferry Me Through Ages　流年啊，渡我漂游千百载

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域：Aedes Elysiae　|　角色：Trailblazer; Cyrene; Screwllum; The Herta; Lygus　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Hero, Sing That Anthem of Creation'' completed

### Story Recap

I reunited with Dan Heng and unexpectedly made contact with Screwllum. He warned us that Amphoreus was actually an experimental ground for the Erudition and was rapidly falling into the Path of Destruction: A Lord Ravager would soon complete its evolution in Amphoreus.

To tackle the crisis together, I stayed behind in Amphoreus as an informant while Dan Heng returned to the Express as planned to seek help. On top of that, Screwllum informed us that March 7th had also been drawn into Amphoreus earlier.

However, the current priority was the "Era Nova." I quickly arrived at the Vortex of Genesis and met with Phainon. The "Era Nova" ritual was supposed to proceed smoothly under the guidance of Lygus, but the appearance of the Flame Reaver once again left the fate of the world hanging in the balance.

We struck down the enemy's mask, only to discover that his face was identical to Phainon's. When his body was pierced by the ceremonial blade, a flood of memories surged forth, revealing the truth about Phainon, the Flame Reaver, and Amphoreus...

It turned out that all of us were trapped in the "eternal recurrences" created by Phainon and Cyrene, and the Flame Reaver was Phainon himself, who had burned out after bearing countless Coreflames. Their goal was simple: To oppose the Aeon and rewrite the world's doomed fate of Destruction.

The eternal recurrences had already played out 33,550,336 times. In this final cycle, I promised Phainon to take on the title of "Deliverer" and save the world in his stead, while he would fulfill the wishes of the countless versions of himself: Tear the sky asunder and confront the Destruction head-on.

Thus, with Phainon's help, time in Amphoreus reset once more, but I became lost in the chaotic flow of time. At the critical moment, Mem, who had regained all her memories, sacrificed herself to send me back to the beginning of time...

**Herta:** Listen up, little one. The Destruction's eyes are gazing upon this place. No, I'm not joking, and no, it's not Droidhead. It's the real Nanook.

> **黑塔：**听好了，小家伙。「毁灭」的目光看向了这里。我没有开玩笑，不是机器头，是纳努克。

**Screwllum:** Time is running out, so I'll explain in terms you understand: this world, Amphoreus, is in fact a testing ground where the Lord Ravager, Irontomb, evolves through self-extrapolation.

> **螺丝咕姆：**时间紧迫，我会用你熟知的概念说明：名为翁法罗斯的世界，实为绝灭大君「铁墓」自我演算、成长的实验场。

**Screwllum:** Hiding in this segmentum unknown to other lifeforms, it has endlessly repeated the process of "Era Nova," seeking a solution that could obliterate the Erudition.

> **螺丝咕姆：**在这片不为人知的星域，它不断重复「再创世」的进程，求解摧毁「智识」的方法。

**Screwllum:** For some reason, the experiment entered a prolonged state of stagnation. However, it has now restarted.

> **螺丝咕姆：**出于某些原因，这场实验曾一度陷入漫长的停滞。但现在，进程已再度启动。

**Screwllum:** Prediction: If effective intervention is not performed, Irontomb will complete its Self-Coronation in 14 system hours and initiate its plan to destroy the entire Path of Erudition.

> **螺丝咕姆：**若无法进行有效干涉，预测：十四个系统时后，铁墓将完成自我加冕，启动对「智识」命途的毁灭计划。

- ▸ How will it destroy the Erudition?

> ▸ 十四个系统时，这也太快了…

**Herta:** The possibilities are endless. Breaking down interstellar communications, destroying the Synesthesia Beacons, returning cosmic civilizations to the dark ages, or even starting the third Anti-Organic War... Any of it would be able to bring the cosmos to its knees.

> **黑塔：**可能性太多了。瓦解星际通讯、破坏联觉信标、让群星重回黑暗时代，甚至掀起第三次反有机战争，为银河带来灭顶之灾。

**Herta:** Simply put, it will tilt all sciences and technologies towards the Destruction. And since Nanook glanced at this place, it means THEY are being dead serious.

> **黑塔：**简而言之，让一切科学和技术倒向「毁灭」——既然纳努克看向了这里，就说明祂是认真的。

- ▸ Hasn't Irontomb been around for a long time?

> ▸ 它要如何毁灭「智识」？

**Herta:** Based on info provided by the IPC, Irontomb probably isn't like Celenova or Zephyro, who are Overlords ascended from human beings. Instead, Irontomb is an algorithmic sequence. It can be anywhere, as long as there is a receptacle for its calculation.

> **黑塔：**从公司提供的情报来看，铁墓多半不是「星啸」或「焚风」那样由人类升格的大君，而是一道算法序列。只要有计算载体，它就无处不在。

**Herta:** The version of Irontomb hiding in Amphoreus is its "source code." And this Scepter is its incubator.

> **黑塔：**潜伏在翁法罗斯的铁墓是「源代码」——这台权杖就是它的培养皿。

- ▸ Fourteen system hours is no time at all...!

> ▸ 「铁墓」不是早就存在了吗？

**Herta:** Yep. Think of it as the program having reached 99.98% completion already... and then it's over.

> **黑塔：**对，你就当已经到了99.98%…只差这么一丁点，进程就要跑完了。

**Screwllum:** Something in the shadows has been driving everything forward: It's the observer named "Lygus." Speculation: He's the Scepter system's Administrator.

> **螺丝咕姆：**有人在幕后推动这一切：名为「来古士」的观测者。推测：他是权杖系统的管理员。

**Herta:** He wants Irontomb to break through the Knowledge Singularity. I don't know why he wants to do it, but he'll remove interferences at any cost. You're the only variable within his world, and you're the only thing he fears.

> **黑塔：**他希望铁墓突破知识奇点。为什么？不知道。但他会不惜一切代价排除干扰。你是世界内部唯一的变量，他唯独忌惮你。

**Herta:** So listen closely, little one. The speed of time is different in Amphoreus compared to the outside. Destruction is just about to arrive, but you can still play Deliverer one last time...

> **黑塔：**听仔细了，小家伙——翁法罗斯内外的时间流速相去甚远，「毁灭」迫在眉睫，但你还有机会当一回救世主……

**Herta:** The cycles have already been broken. You've entered what is theoretically the last cycle Amphoreus will ever be able to run, and Irontomb will break out of its prison as soon as the process of "genesis" is triggered once more.

> **黑塔：**循环已被打破，你进入了翁法罗斯理论上的最后一次回归。只要再完成一次「创世」进程，铁墓就会破壳而出。

**Herta:** But now there's an abnormality within the experiment. You know what I'm talking about. One of the model's twelve factors — that is, one of the twelve demigods — destroyed himself using the power of the Destruction.

> **黑塔：**但实验出现了异常，你知道原因：计算中的十二因子——我是说，十二名半神里，有一位通过「毁灭」破坏了自身。

**Herta:** Phainon's signal disappeared from the cycles the moment Nanook cast THEIR gaze this way. That means Worldbearing's computational logic is currently flawed and bugged, yet it's the factor directly related to the process of "genesis."

> **黑塔：**纳努克投来视线的同时，「白厄」的信号从轮回中消失了。现在「负世」的逻辑出现了漏洞，而它正和「创世」进程直接关联。

- ▸ Phainon sacrificed himself to create an opportunity for us.

> ▸ 白厄用生命为我们创造了机会。

- ▸ He entrusted the duty of Worldbearing to me.

> ▸ 他把「负世」的使命交给了我。

- ▸ Phainon didn't die. He's still fighting.

> ▸ 白厄没有死，他仍在反抗。

**Herta:** That old Intellitron will definitely do all he can to debug this error, so you have to take this chance to claim Kephale's Coreflame. Don't let him have his way. The key to suppressing Irontomb lies in whether we can rewrite the purpose of "Era Nova."

> **黑塔：**你必须抓住机会，把刻法勒的火种牢牢握在自己手里。智械哥一定会想方设法填上这道漏洞，别让他得逞，能否改变「再创世」的意义，是我们对抗铁墓的关键。

**Screwllum:** There's one more preparation we must make. Lygus's behavior has been quite abnormal. He's never interfered in the actions of Titans or the demigods throughout the whole experiment.

> **螺丝咕姆：**逻辑：「权杖」有着极其严密的自主协议，管理员也必须接受限制。在翁法罗斯，这道终极协议有另一重命名——

> **来古士：**此地是翁法罗斯与现实宇宙的交界，是鄙人观察这场实验的观众席，您亦可称之为…「神话之外」。

### Explore the Era of the First Flame-Chase Journey

> *People say this is a cruel era stained with golden blood and crimson, an era of catastrophe where tsunamis rage and mountains crumble, an era of tyranny where corrupt rulers trample the law, a dark era where both gods and their faithful are slaughtered. This is the era of the First Flame-Chase Journey, an era with a scorching sun that burns with blinding light. And you must gaze directly into it, asking it to illuminate the fire you seek.*

33,550,337th eternal recurrence, Light Calendar 3960

"One hundred years ago, the flames of war consumed Okhema."

"The outland forces broke through the dawn. Golden blood flowed with greed, and enemies descended with Kephale's eternal slumber, painting the holy city's walls in scarlet."

"Its generals had vanished, and the Elders prostrated on the ground. The Throne of Worlds kept silent to the pleas of the people..."

"Until the heaven and earth cracked open... not the gods answering the people's prayers, but a legion rising from the sea of blood."

"Dawncloud's spires fell in the sulfur rains, and the Legion's commander stood atop the broken idols and roared..."

City's Savior General: Compatriots, no more kneeling! Not to brutal tyrants, and not to dead or dying gods!

City's Savior General: Those rebels have twisted the prophecy and ignited the flames of war in order to stoke fear in your hearts!

"A Chrysos Heir she stood. Divine blood soaked into the earth, quelling the flames beneath her feet."

City's Savior General: Their golden blood wrote of a tragic fate. Mine shall overturn it!

"Her crown burned bright, outshining the fires consuming the entire city. She raised her scepter high, and the people gazed in the direction she pointed..."

City's Savior General: My blood flows in you now! Heroic compatriots, follow me, and we'll seize the reins of fate!

City's Savior General: Drive the enemy from Kephale's dawn! Smother their flames with their own dead! Win glory! Win peace! Let history sing your deeds!

"The first to dance was a maiden following closely behind, weaving through carnage like a fish in the sea."

### Speak with Aglaea and Tribbie

> *People say this is a cruel era stained with golden blood and crimson, an era of catastrophe where tsunamis rage and mountains crumble, an era of tyranny where corrupt rulers trample the law, a dark era where both gods and their faithful are slaughtered. This is the era of the First Flame-Chase Journey, an era with a scorching sun that burns with blinding light. And you must gaze directly into it, asking it to illuminate the fire you seek.*

*(Talk to Labienus and Seneca, optional)*

> **昔涟：**如果有时间，要不要也接触一下？

**Aglaea:** The one from beyond the sky has arrived, and destiny continues to be fulfilled. The Trial of Romance is also close at hand...

> **阿格莱雅：**天外之人已至，命运逐一应验。想来，「浪漫」的试炼也近在眼前了……

**Tribbie:** Please don't worry... We'll sing you a lullaby! Little Agy be a good girl and open your heart...

> **缇宝：**不要难过…让*我们*唱首童谣吧！阿雅乖乖，把心门开开……

> **阿格莱雅：**…我已经不是小孩子啦，吾师。

What exactly is the Imperator doing...?

Another round of Flame-Chase is starting?

I get to see the Imperator again!

**Hysilens:** Little fish from beyond the sky, did you rest well?

> **海瑟音：**天外之海的鱼儿们，在湖中歇息得如何？

**Hysilens:** There is still some time before the meeting begins. Rather than listening to the whispers of the delegates, why don't you get to know your future comrades first?

> **海瑟音：**凯撒与城邦代表们即将入场，若准备妥帖，便随我一同游去议会吧。

*(If all Chrysos Heirs have been talked to)*

**Hysilens:** The Imperator and the delegates are about to arrive. If you are ready, please follow me to the meeting.

> **海瑟音：**天外之海的鱼儿们，在湖中歇息得如何？

- ▸ About your role...

**Hysilens:** I'm the Knight Commander of Okhema, General of the Flame-Chase Army, and the Imperator's servant...

> **海瑟音：**距离会议开始还有些时间，与其去听城邦代表们的嘁嚓细语，不如先认识下未来的战友吧。

**Hysilens:** But beneath these shiny scales engraved with complicated titles? I am just a swordfish at the Imperator's side.

> **海瑟音：**那就来吧，凯撒已为你们留好了席位。

- ▸ About the Imperator...

**Hysilens:** As you've witnessed, she is the empress of Okhema, the one who ended the Chrysos War, and the leader of the Chrysos Heirs.

> **海瑟音：**我是奥赫玛的骑士统领，逐火军的大将，也是凯撒的近侍——

> **海瑟音：**除却这些刻着繁复名号的鳞片，我只是凯撒身侧的一尾游鱼，一柄剑旗。

**Hysilens:** The Chrysos Heirs here constitute the elite among the Flame-Chase Army...

> **海瑟音：**如你所见，她是奥赫玛的女皇，黄金战争的终结者，黄金裔的领袖。

**Hysilens:** "Dux Helkolithist" Apollonius and "Dux Carminum" Verginia serve as the army's advisor and historian, respectfully. One offers advice and counsel to the Imperator while the other writes verses of her supreme glory.

**Hysilens:** "Dux Fragoris" Labienus and "Dux Brumalis" Seneca are both formidable warriors, clearing the way for the Flame-Chase with steel and storm.

> **海瑟音：**在这里的黄金裔，皆是逐火军中的佼佼者——

**Hysilens:** Dux Fatorum, the Holy Maiden who interprets the prophecy... Only the one named Tribbie remains in the city. The Imperator once took them as hostages in order to unify the prophecies of the world... But they are now Okhema's most vital prophets.

> **海瑟音：**「曳石爵」阿波罗尼与「吟风爵」维吉妮娅，他们是逐火军的参谋与史官，一人为凯撒出谋献计，一人将她的无上功绩书作诗篇。

**Hysilens:** "Dux Goldweaver" Aglaea... Well, she dislikes it when people talk about her behind her back. You should get to know her in person.

> **海瑟音：**「断锋爵」拉比努斯与「冬霖爵」塞涅卡，他们是逐火军中强大的战士，用剑刃与风暴扫清逐火的前路。

**Cyrene:** Since there's still some time, let's get to know our new friends here~

**Hysilens:** Alright. There's no rush.

**Epos Representative:** Good sir from Paphas, what do you think the Imperator is planning, calling this meeting so early? My homeland can't endure anymore...

**Paphas Representative:** Who knows? First she wants to hunt Titans, now she says there are even greater calamities... Her madness is the greatest calamity.

**Ladon Representative:** Exactly! Chrysos Heirs, "Deliverers"... What about us ordinary folk? How much are we worth in her eyes?

**Hysilens:** Yes, similar acts have occurred, and not just once. However, whether history will repeat itself... you'll soon see.

> **海瑟音：**凯撒确实做过类似的事，而且不止一次。不过，至于历史是否会重演…二位稍后便会明白了。

**Hysilens:** Those are the two spots reserved for you. Please take your seats.

*Representatives chattering*

**Hysilens:** Ladies and gentlemen, silence please. The Imperator has arrived before her faithful council, and the alliance meeting is about to begin.

> **海瑟音：**诸君，请肃静。凯撒已驾临她忠诚的议会，同盟会议即将开始。

**Epos Representative:** All rise! In the names of Talanton and Kephale, the Okhema Alliance swears upon the gods...

> **埃普斯代表：**全体起立！以塔兰顿与刻法勒之名，奥赫玛同盟向诸神宣誓——

**Cerydra:** Cease the pageantry. I pray not to the vanquished.

> **刻律德菈：**不必多此一举了。凯撒不向败者祈祷。

**Epos Representative:** What...

> **埃普斯代表：**什么……

**Cerydra:** Kephale, of course, needs no further explanation. As for the subjugation of the "Law," at the Entry Hour two days past, a scout should've brought the news to all of you...

> **刻律德菈：**刻法勒自不必多说。两日前的门扉时，斥候本应准时抵达奥赫玛，为诸位带来「律法」已被征服的捷报……

**Cerydra:** Unfortunately, guards found his corpse among the rocks not far from the city.

> **刻律德菈：**但令人遗憾的是，戍卫在城外不远处的乱石堆中发现了他的遗体。

**Cerydra:** Moreover, the poor soul was buried with a Paphas curved blade. It pains me to imagine what agonies this loyal warrior endured before his death...

> **刻律德菈：**不仅如此，还有一柄帕福斯弯刀给那可怜人做了陪葬。我实在难以想象，这位忠诚的战士临死前受了多少折磨……

**Cerydra:** Delegate from Paphas, do you have any insights?

> **刻律德菈：**帕福斯代表，你对此有何高见？

**Paphas Representative:** I—I know nothing of this...

> **帕福斯代表：**什、什么？我从未得知此事……

**Cerydra:** The evidence is irrefutable, so there is no need to waste your breath. However, let us set this trial aside for the time being...

> **刻律德菈：**铁证如山，不必白费口舌了。不过，这场审判先按下不表——

**Cerydra:** Witness! Even within the most sacred and dignified Council, there were those among us who wished to draw their blades, brazenly challenging the alliance, seeking to shatter our hard-earned peace and unity...

> **刻律德菈：**诸君已经看到了：在这世上最神圣、最庄严的议院中，就在我们之间，竟有人想要将屠刀挥向对方，公然挑衅同盟，摧毁来之不易的团结与和平……

**Cerydra:** Fortunately, I uncovered this plot. Now, to protect this beautiful city and her people, the Imperator's legions are ready...

> **刻律德菈：**不幸中的万幸，我发现了这一切。现在，为了守卫这座美丽城市和她的人民，凯撒的军团已经做好准备——

**Cerydra:** They will escort each of you to the Temple of the Three Fates, where you will receive the highest level of protection the alliance can offer.

> **刻律德菈：**——各位将会在他们的护送下，迁往命运三相殿，并在那里接受同盟最高规格的保护。

**Cerydra:** Then, the legions' warriors will join me in embarking on the campaign against Phagousa.

> **刻律德菈：**而后，军团的勇士将随我一道，踏上讨伐法吉娜的征程。

**Paphas Representative:** This... this is clearly a trap!

> **帕福斯代表：**这…这分明是个圈套！

**Ladon Representative:** Imperator! Did you summon us here just to trample on the dignity of your allies!?

> **拉冬代表：**凯撒！你叫我们前来，只是为了践踏盟友的尊严么！

*Representatives chattering*

> **昔涟：**场面变得焦灼起来了呀……

> **拉冬代表：**同胞们，你们都看清她的嘴脸了！依照奥赫玛旧律，只有经三分之二多数元老同意，军团才能执行总帅的命令……

**Cerydra:** Heh, the ancient laws! Didn't you destroy them long ago? You defected during the First Siege of Okhema, voted unanimously in favor of the Council of Elders' restructuring, and just yesterday, you desecrated them with a blood-stained blade...

> **刻律德菈：**呵，旧律！你们不早就把它毁了么？你们在奥赫玛第一次围城战时临阵倒戈，在重组元老院的决议中全部投下赞成票，还在昨天亲手用染血的刀锋亵渎了它——

**Cerydra:** The decaying old laws fell with Talanton and are no longer deserving of respect. But, if you still insist on proclaiming yourselves defenders of the "Law"...

> **刻律德菈：**——腐朽的旧律已随着塔兰顿一同陨落，不再配得尊敬。但，倘若诸位还坚持标榜自己捍卫「律法」……

**Cerydra:** Then be silent, and offer up the highest respect to this Coreflame in my hand.

> **刻律德菈：**那便就此噤声，向我手中这枚火种献上最高的敬意。

> **昔涟：**「律法」的火种，果然在她手里……

**Hysilens:** The Flame-Chase Army's hunt for the Ocean Titan begins today. In order to prepare for war, all allied city-states must surrender command of their militaries and pay all war taxes by the end of the Month of Evernight.

> **海瑟音：**逐火军对海洋泰坦的狩猎，即日开始。限同盟诸邦于长夜月内交出军权，并缴齐战税，以便筹备战事。

**Cerydra:** At this moment, I would also like to introduce to everyone the newest honored guest to join our alliance...

> **刻律德菈：**以此契机，并请各位见过我同盟新至的贵客——

> **刻律德菈：**「岁月」神谕中揭示的「救世主」，开拓者，已从天外降临。我等将在救世主的助力下，击碎满溢之杯——

**Cerydra:** And return Phagousa's Coreflame to the holy city!

> **刻律德菈：**令法吉娜的火种，归于圣城！

**Ladon Representative:** Absurd... simply absurd!

**Ladon Representative:** A Deliverer from beyond the sky? This is just like that prophecy about slaying gods... It's a lie you've used to grow your power!

**Cerydra:** Hah...

**Cerydra:** Are there any other objections?

**Cerydra:** Then, I bid you all farewell... Meeting adjourned.

> **刻律德菈：**部署棋局、左右下注，这运筹帷幄的游戏，反倒能为此世沉重的命运平添一抹亮色，就像统治和征服本就伴随着牺牲。

> **昔涟：**和泰坦的战争，也算是牺牲的一部分吗？

> **刻律德菈：**我问你，救世主：于这翁法罗斯的天幕之外，存在名为「星神」的宏伟神祇——此事为真？

**Cerydra:** Ah... the word "fact" has never been so intoxicating.

**Cerydra:** Ah... that certainly captivates my interest.

> **刻律德菈：**啊…那的确令人神往。

> **刻律德菈：**那么，请再告诉我：你将如何运用此种伟力，引领世人……

**Cerydra:** In the future you envision, where will our Flame-Chase Journey lead?

> **刻律德菈：**在你描绘的未来中，我们逐火的征程将通向何方？

**Cerydra:** ...Oh? What you require now... is the power of "Law"?

> **昔涟：**如果能让他们介入翁法罗斯的「律法」，就有机会改变未来的命运，扭转这个世界注定的结局。

**Cerydra:** Ha... Hahahaha!

> **刻律德菈：**哈…哈哈哈！

**Cerydra:** Very good, Deliverer, you have courage! To say such presumptuous things, even knowing that the inheritor of the "Law" stands before you...

> **刻律德菈：**很好，救世主，你很有胆识！明知「律法」的继承者就站在你眼前，竟还敢如此放肆——

**Cyrene:** You mean...

> **昔涟：**您的意思是……

> **刻律德菈：**换做从前，若有人胆敢觊觎凯撒神权，多半只会落得碎尸万段的下场。但如今，我们的视线已能越过这片苟延残喘的大地……

**Cerydra:** Our journey... should no longer be confined below this stubborn sky.

> **刻律德菈：**我们的征程…也不应再被这顽固的天幕制约。

**Cerydra:** Alas, that fickle Ocean Titan has obstructed the spirit water leading to the Vortex of Genesis, hindering my ascension. If the Coreflame of "Law" cannot be returned, your path to Deliverance will never be realized...

> **刻律德菈：**无奈，那反复无常的海洋泰坦阻断了通往创世涡心的灵水，给我的晋升之路徒增阻碍。若「律法」火种不能归还，你们指明的救世之路亦无法实现……

**Cerydra:** Now that things have come to this, we must resume our once-stagnant journey... and force the waves that block our way to recede.

> **刻律德菈：**事至如今，我们也该再起停滞的征程…令拦路的浪潮退去了。

**Cyrene:** So... you agree to cooperate with us?

> **昔涟：**所以…您答应与我们合作了？

**Cerydra:** Cooperate? Forgive me, but I never "cooperate" with anyone...

> **刻律德菈：**合作？恕我直言，我从来不和任何人*合作*……

**Cerydra:** Though, I will permit you to join me as subjects and together, we will forge the most perfect "Law."

> **刻律德菈：**但，我允许诸位以王臣的身份与我踏上征程——随我铸就最完美的「律法」。

**Cyrene:** This is...

> **昔涟：**这是……

**Cerydra:** The "Flame-Chase Medallion," a symbol of the Flame-Chase Army. Every noble in Okhema carries one.

> **刻律德菈：**「逐火铭牌」，逐火军的身份象征。奥赫玛每位王爵都持有此物。

**Cerydra:** This medallion represents the will of the Imperator herself, and you may act freely within the city with it. Before we embark on the crusade against the Ocean Titan, you should rest in Okhema for a time.

> **刻律德菈：**见到此牌就等于见到凯撒本人，你们可以在城中随意行动。启程征伐海洋泰坦前，两位就在奥赫玛暂时休憩吧。

> **刻律德菈：**……

> **刻律德菈：**如欧洛尼斯说的那般，那两人来了。也正如你所说，他们展现出了对「律法」的渴求……

**Cerydra:** At least, this proves that my judgment was right: Sparing your life was the correct decision.

> **刻律德菈：**至少，这证明我的判断没有错：留你一命是正确的选择。

**Lygus:** Heh, you've once again confirmed that your wisdom is no empty boast.

**Lygus:** Now, another choice lies before you: With whom shall you ally to tip the scales of "Law": me, or that "Deliverer"?

> **来古士：**如今，又一个选择摆在您的面前：该与何人协作，让「律法」的天秤偏向哪方——我，抑或那位「救世主」？

**Cerydra:** ...

> **刻律德菈：**……

**Cerydra:** Remind me again, Antikytheran, what kind of future do these two paths lead to, in your view?

> **刻律德菈：**再提醒我一次吧，安提基色拉人——在你的视野里，这两条路分别通向何种未来？

**Lygus:** I shall tell you.

> **来古士：**遵命。

**Lygus:** You are entitled to know that the "Deliverer" from beyond the sky is not a wholly wicked being. But from the very start, his/her stance has been completely at odds with Amphoreus.

> **来古士：**您有权明白，那位来自天外的「救世主」并非十恶不赦之人。不过从一开始，他/她的立场就与翁法罗斯全然不一。

**Lygus:** The road they have chosen will bury Amphoreus's grand aspirations: This world which follows the Path of "Destruction" will inevitably stray far from the glory it was meant to enjoy...

> **来古士：**他们/她们选择的道路将掩埋翁法罗斯的宏愿：这座行于「毁灭」命途的世界，必会远离它本应享有的荣光……

**Lygus:** But on the path I have revealed to you, the people of Amphoreus will slay their Creator in the name of the Imperator and conquer the worlds beyond the sky.

> **来古士：**而在我为您揭示的道路上：翁法罗斯的众生将以凯撒之名弑杀造物尊神，征服天外万界。

**Lygus:** You will command the mighty beast known as "Irontomb," bringing waves of war and conquest to the universe. Beings across the cosmos will bow before you, offering their allegiance.

> **来古士：**您将驱使名为「铁墓」的巨兽，为宇宙带去伟大的战潮与征服——寰宇众生都将拜于座前，对您俯首称臣。

**Cerydra:** So... what's the price?

> **刻律德菈：**那么…代价是什么？

**Lygus:** The price is insignificant. All that is needed is for you to sacrifice that fragile, incomplete humanity to the Destruction...

> **来古士：**代价微不足道。只需将那脆弱、残缺的人性于「毁灭」中献祭——

**Lygus:** As a Chrysos Heir of the prophecy... you have already chosen to abandon it, have you not?

> **来古士：**身为神谕中的黄金裔…您早已决定将它舍弃了，不是么？

**Cerydra:** Hah...

> **刻律德菈：**呵……

**Cerydra:** Off you go, Theoros. I have already made my decision.

> **刻律德菈：**退下吧，神礼观众。我心中已有定数。

**Cerydra:** Once I ignite the Coreflame of "Law" and burn away the old, outdated rules...

> **刻律德菈：**待我点亮「律法」的火种，焚尽旧日陈腐的规则……

**Cerydra:** Amphoreus will embark on a journey across the stars.

> **刻律德菈：**届时，翁法罗斯必能踏上遍布群星的征程。

### "Relax" at Marmoreal Palace

> *The myth has drifted from its destined course. If fate is written in the stars, then their gravity pulls not just the sun, but the very tides at your feet. The stars may be untouchable, but the tide isn't. Stand in its flow and feel how cosmic forces lap at your ankles. That's the ocean whispering its worries, wave by wave, shore to soul.*

> **昔涟：**…有了，留意下附近有没有被垫高的座位吧。

**Hysilens:** Gray fry, little sea hare...

> **海瑟音：**灰鱼儿，小海兔……

**Hysilens:** Please come over and share the delicacies of the deep sea with me.

> **海瑟音：**请来这里，与我共享海底的奇珍吧。

*(Talk to Hysilens)*

*(If no Chrysos Heirs have been talked to)*

*(If one or more Chrysos Heirs have been talked to)*

**Hysilens:** You two have been wandering around the venue as if you have something on your mind. Have the attendants not taken care of you well?

> **海瑟音：**两位四处徘徊，也不与旁人交杯，莫非是侍从招待不周？

> **昔涟：**不会不会。我们很享受！只是…见凯撒迟迟未到，心里难免有些惴惴不安。

**Hysilens:** Don't worry. I know what you've been waiting for.

> **海瑟音：**别心急，我知道两位在等什么。

**Hysilens:** Come. Sit with me for a while, will you? I'd like to share a personal secret with you.

> **海瑟音：**来，陪我坐一小会儿吧？我有个私人的秘密想同你们分享。

**Hysilens:** See here...

> **海瑟音：**请看……

**Hysilens:** This is Phagousa's Dew of Divine Blood, the sweetest of all the twelve bottles.

> **海瑟音：**这是法吉娜的神血蜜露，十二宝瓶中最为甜美的一樽。

**Hysilens:** On the night before such a historical event, I'd like to share this marvelous drink with the "Deliverer."

> **海瑟音：**在这历史变革的前夜，如此奇珍，我想邀「救世主」共饮。

**Cyrene:** Doesn't this count as bringing in outside food, (Trailblazer)? I knew that security check was targeted at us.

> **昔涟：**开拓者，这算自带酒水了吧？果然，安检就是针对我们的。

**Hysilens:** What's the matter, guests?

**Cyrene:** Oh! We're... simply overwhelmed by such an honor.

> **昔涟：**有些…受宠若惊呢。

> **海瑟音：**是担心我在蜜露中布了毒饵？那么，请让我自证清白。

**Hysilens:** ...

> **海瑟音：**……

Hysilens raises the goblet of Honeydew to her lips, deliberately arching her neck so you could watch the liquid's descent down her throat as she swallows.

**Hysilens:** See? It's completely safe.

> **海瑟音：**看吧，十分安全。

**Cyrene:** I feel like Miss Hysilens wants to talk to us about something... Why don't we go along with her for now?

> **昔涟：**总觉得海瑟音小姐想和我们谈些什么…先配合她吧？

- ▸ Then I'll gladly accept it.
- ▸ I want a new cup for myself.

**Hysilens:** Hah. You're a pretty sharp fish.

> **海瑟音：**呵，真是条机敏的鱼儿。

- ▸ Minors shouldn't drink...

**Hysilens:** Worry not. Honeydew is not alcohol. It won't get you drunk.

> **海瑟音：**安心。蜜露并非酒酿，不会令人迷醉。

**Hysilens:** On the contrary, it can make your mind... even sharper.

> **海瑟音：**恰恰相反，它能让你更加清醒。

The clay cup touches your lips in the blink of an eye. She is indeed the imperial blade of the Imperator and the swiftest swordmaster in Amphoreus.

**Hysilens:** Please. Have a taste.

> **海瑟音：**还请，细细品尝。

**Hysilens:** Would you like some too, little sea hare?

> **海瑟音：**小海兔，你也一起？

> **昔涟：**人、人家还是自己来吧……

**Cyrene:** Glug... glug...

> **昔涟：**咕…咕……

**Hysilens:** How is it? Sweet enough?

> **海瑟音：**如何，甜吗？

- ▸ It's not bad...

**Hysilens:** As long as you like it.

> **海瑟音：**喜欢便好。

- ▸ It has a... very complex taste.

**Hysilens:** Calm at times, tempestuous at others... That's the taste of the "Ocean." It'll do you good to understand Phagousa's fickleness before we set off to face their waves.

> **海瑟音：**时而平静、时而猛烈，这便是「海洋」的滋味。在启程面对法吉娜的波涛前，你理应先熟悉它的善变。

**Hysilens:** Just like how you are about to understand the secrets within this Dew...

> **海瑟音：**就像此时此刻，两位将见证这蜜露中蕴含的秘密……

**Hysilens:** Now, please turn around. Very. Very. Quietly. Don't make a single noise.

> **海瑟音：**现在，请转过头去，轻轻地——不要发出任何响动。

- ▸ The Imperator!? When did she...
- ▸ Lygus! I knew he'd be here!
- ▸ What... What did you do...?

**Cyrene:** Ahh...!

> **昔涟：**呀……

**Hysilens:** The Dew of other gods all intoxicate humans, but the God of Banquets' Dew...

> **海瑟音：**其余诸神的蜜露皆会引人沉醉，唯独欢宴之神的蜜露……

**Hysilens:** Is an "antidote" that can awaken humans from a sea siren's enchanting song.

> **海瑟音：**是能将人们从海妖的迷魂歌谣中唤醒的「解药」。

**Hysilens:** Since the start of this banquet, all the guests have been under the spell of my song. None of them can see the Imperator and that Theoros.

> **海瑟音：**打从宴会开始，众宾客就被我的歌声催眠，他们看不见凯撒和那位神礼官。

**Hysilens:** But you two... I wish for you to see the truth.

> **海瑟音：**但二位——我希望你们拥有清醒的视野。

**Cyrene:** Is this... also what the Imperator wants?

> **昔涟：**这是…凯撒的授意？

**Hysilens:** This is my personal request. I... can't bring myself to trust that Theoros.

> **海瑟音：**是我的私人请求。我…无法信任那位神礼官。

**Hysilens:** The Flame-Chase Army changed after he descended with Oronyx's divination and brought the prophecy of the stars to Okhema. His seductive whispers harbor something malicious within and will ensnare those of us blinded by ambition.

> **海瑟音：**自从他和欧洛尼斯的神谕一同降临，将群星的预言带给奥赫玛，逐火军就变了。他的低语难以抗拒，包藏祸心，令野心障目的人们一一踩入陷阱。

**Hysilens:** You are the "Deliverers" of the prophecy and know about him far more than I do. Perhaps only you can find traces of the truth from his actions and words...

> **海瑟音：**你们是预言中的「救世主」，对他的了解远胜过我。或许，只有你们能从其人言行中发觉一丝端倪……

**Hysilens:** Then, we can show the Imperator the right path forward.

> **海瑟音：**然后，为凯撒展示正确的道路。

**Cyrene:** But... isn't this acting behind her back?

> **昔涟：**可这么做…不是自作主张吗？

**Hysilens:** Amphoreus has become far too weak. Even heroic actions protecting her from harm must be conducted under the water's surface. I am the Imperator's blade, and I must clear the way for her while remaining concealed in the torrents...

> **海瑟音：**翁法罗斯已经太过脆弱，就连捍卫她的英勇行为也必须藏在水面下。身为凯撒的锋刃，我必须在暗流中为她开辟前路……

**Hysilens:** Then, ensure I'm pointing my sword towards the true enemy.

> **海瑟音：**然后，把剑指向真正的敌人。

**Cyrene:** ...

> **昔涟：**……

> **昔涟：**海瑟音小姐，能答应我一个请求吗？

> **昔涟：**开拓者此行前来，正是为了阻止那个坏人的阴谋。我们的立场是一致的。

**Cyrene:** But as to the decision the Imperator must make... (Trailblazer) has no wish to transgress her authority. We just want to tell her everything we know.

> **昔涟：**但凯撒要做的选择…开拓者从无僭越之意。我们只想把知道的一切坦诚相告，毫无保留。

> **昔涟：**我相信，凯撒自会明辨是非。

> **海瑟音：**当然，我从不怀疑二位的忠诚，也定会如实传达。

> **海瑟音：**但前提是，你我能在这场拉锯中取得优势。

**Hysilens:** Now, return to the feast. Bring this cup with you and keep your distance. Pretend you are still immersed in the sea siren's fantasia...

> **海瑟音：**现在，回到宴会中去吧。带上这酒杯，保持距离，假装你们还沉浸在海妖的幻境中……

**Hysilens:** And I'll control the moisture in the air. That way, you'll be able to listen to sounds from afar, as if you are a whale of the depths, and hear every word of their conversation.

> **海瑟音：**我会控制水汽，让你们能像深海鲸兽那般听见远方的声音，听清交谈的细节。

**Hysilens:** I wish you a victory in this first skirmish.

> **海瑟音：**祝二位，旗开得胜。

### Undercover mission! Eavesdrop on the Imperator's secret talks

**Cyrene:** It's... the exciting and thrilling undercover part of the adventure!

Dux Gladiorum still sings her bewitching tune. Without the fine wine she offered, you might have drowned in the tide of her honeyed voice, just like the rest of the guests.

> **昔涟：**这里！混进人群中去吧？

**"Dux Brumalis" Seneca:** Where's the Imperator? Still not here? That little imp is always acting so high and mighty towards everyone! Today, I will... I will...!

> **「冬霖爵」塞涅卡：**凯撒人呢，怎么还不现身？那小矮子，平日对谁都一副颐指气使的样子！今天我就要、就要……

**Lygus:** Though she has succumbed to drunkenness, she still does not dare challenge your authority.

> **来古士：**即便已被醉意战胜，此人仍不敢挑战您的权威哪。

**Cerydra:** A criticism of my height is a grave enough insult already. She should be dismembered.

> **刻律德菈：**胆敢妄议我的身高，仅这一点就足够将她碎尸万段。

**Lygus:** Do you often test the loyalty of your subjects like this?

> **来古士：**您时常以这种方式考验臣子的忠诚么？

**Cerydra:** You jest. The Imperator does not need blind loyalty. As long as one can prove they have value, I am willing to be lenient towards them.

> **刻律德菈：**可笑。凯撒不需要愚忠之人，只需证明自己尚有价值，我便愿意网开一面。

**Cerydra:** Let's cut to the chase, Theoros. If I pass the trial and grasp this world's "Law" in my hands, what will you do then?

> **刻律德菈：**说正事吧，神礼官：倘若我通过试炼，将此世「律法」执握在手。下一步，你打算怎么做？

**Lygus:** The answer is simple. I will connect Amphoreus with the stars...

> **来古士：**答案显而易见，我会将翁法罗斯与群星相连……

**Cerydra:** Don't waste my time. Be precise. Be specific. I won't ask a second time.

> **刻律德菈：**别说废话。简洁、具体，我不会提醒第二次。

**Lygus:** Allow me to adjust my statement: I will correct the regulations within the old "Law" that sealed off Amphoreus, and merge the black tide with the torrents of Era Nova.

> **来古士：**容我更正：我会修正旧日「律法」中封闭翁法罗斯的条约，并将黑潮汇入再创世的涡流。

**Lygus:** Then, we will be able to decimate the forces beyond the sky that are trying to annihilate Irontomb, and sound the trumpet for the endless campaign you will then embark on.

> **来古士：**如此，我们便能痛击企图扼杀「铁墓」的天外势力，为您将要踏上的无尽征途吹响启程的号角。

**Cerydra:** But you previously said that Irontomb is unrivaled in power across the stars. Who would dare to annihilate it?

> **刻律德菈：**可你分明说过，铁墓在星间无可匹敌，何人胆敢扬言扼杀？

**Lygus:** There is a federation of warships named the Xianzhou Alliance, warlike and old-fashioned. A cult named the Genius Society, loosely organized but steeped in abject insanity. There's also the Astral Express, a group of lawless vagrants...

> **来古士：**名为「仙舟联盟」的战舰联邦，好战、守旧；名为「天才俱乐部」的教团，松散、却极端疯狂；还有「星穹列车」，一群无法的流浪者……

**Lygus:** That so-called "Deliverer" is part of that last group. Those vagabonds mistakenly believe they can connect the stars through alliances instead of conquest, but history has already given them a tough lesson.

> **来古士：**所谓「救世主」也是其中一员。这群流民误以为自己能串联群星，以同盟替代征服，然而历史早已给出当头棒喝。

**Lygus:** There are countless other organizations besides, but when the Lord Ravager sweeps across the cosmos, they will certainly all bow before your empire of Destruction.

> **来古士：**诸如此类，不胜枚举。但待到绝灭大君席卷寰宇，他们都将臣服于您的「毁灭」帝国。

**Cerydra:** ...

> **刻律德菈：**……

> **「冬霖爵」塞涅卡：**奥赫玛盟军…无往不胜！

**Cerydra:** ...If those civilizations are not as feeble as you describe, then they may still have some value in testing the quality of my blade.

> **刻律德菈：**…倘若那些文明不像你所说的这般虚弱，倒还有及锋一试的价值。

**Cerydra:** That being said, even the smallest and weakest city-states should build an obelisk to the Imperator, so that their people can constantly recall the glory of her conquests.

> **刻律德菈：**不过，再渺小的城邦也应当为凯撒树立方尖碑，以便世人随时想起她征服时的威光。

**Cerydra:** Walk a little longer with me, Theoros.

> **刻律德菈：**随我再走几步吧，神礼官。

**Hysilens:** Gray fry? Little sea hare? It seems like they're still talking. Get closer. I'll keep carrying their voices to you...

> **海瑟音：**灰鱼儿，小海兔，他们似乎还在商议。再靠近些吧，我会继续为你们传音……

**Lygus:** Therefore, the wisest decision would be to hand him/her over to me.

**Hysilens:** As I expected... That Theoros's schemes have been revealed.

**Cerydra:** Dux Gladiorum, Hysilens... She used the sea siren's song to immerse the crowds in an illusion and conceal her whereabouts. She then worked with those from beyond the sky to spy on confidential matters, hoping to rob the Coreflame by force and harm me, the Imperator!

> **刻律德菈：**「剑旗爵」海瑟音——此人用海妖的歌谣令众人深陷幻觉，隐匿行踪。并伙同天外之人窃听机密，意图强夺火种，谋害凯撒！

> **刻律德菈：**在橄榄树荫下，他们说奥赫玛绝不会臣服；在冰冷的死雾中，他们说哀地里亚绝不会臣服；在千门万径起点，他们说雅努萨波利斯绝不会臣服……

> **来古士：**您仍然没能看清自己的立场。请容我重申——

**Lygus:** I am giving you a simple choice: To collaborate or to be destroyed. There is no other way.

> **来古士：**我给予您的选择，简截了当：合作，或是毁灭，再无其他。

**Cerydra:** Shameless behavior. If that were true, you would have killed me long ago.

> **刻律德菈：**大言不惭。倘若你能，何不早早杀之后快？

**Cerydra:** You, in fact, cannot kill me. I guessed it the first time I beheaded you: Because of the "Law," you cannot be completely destroyed, and the same is true of the Chrysos Heirs.

> **刻律德菈：**因为你做不到。第一次斩下你的头颅时，我便早有预料：出于「律法」，黄金裔无法将你彻底消灭，反之亦然。

**Lygus:** ...

> **来古士：**……

**Cerydra:** The more you bluff now, the more you are proving that conjecture to be true.

> **刻律德菈：**现在，你越是虚张声势，便越能印证这一事实。

**Lygus:** What magnificent foresight. I once regarded this precious quality of yours as a sure sign of my ultimate victory, but now I have to admit that I made a mistake.

> **来古士：**卓越的眼界。我曾将您这宝贵的品质视作我必胜的信号。但如今，我不得不承认自己的错误——

**Lygus:** Yet, as a mere mortal, how will you contend against the Theoros?

> **来古士：**区区凡人，要如何才能与「神礼观众」抗衡？

**Cerydra:** We are both slaves to hubris, but do you know what differentiates us, Lycurgus? I will never be blinded by my pride.

> **刻律德菈：**你我皆是傲慢的俘虏。但吕枯耳戈斯，知道我们的区别在哪吗？我不会因傲慢变得盲目。

**Cerydra:** If I wish to break the scales' balance...

> **刻律德菈：**想要打破天秤平衡——

**Cerydra:** I only need to appeal to external forces and use them for my own ends.

> **刻律德菈：**——诉诸外力，为己所用便是。

**Herta:** Finally, we get to meet face-to-face... Senior Society Member.

> **黑塔：**终于见到真家伙了啊，*前辈*。

**Screwllum:** I offer sincere acknowledgements for your many past achievements, but I must deeply regret your current perspective.

> **螺丝咕姆：**为您过去达成的诸多成就，我向您致敬；但对于您当下的立场，我深表遗憾。

**Lygus:** ...

> **来古士：**……

**Lygus:** Trust me when I say that you aren't the only one feeling regret right now, Screwllum.

> **来古士：**请相信我，螺丝咕姆。此时此刻，深感遗憾的不止你一人。

**Cerydra:** So you two are the allies from beyond the sky the Deliverer spoke of, and why he/she demanded the divine authority of "Law."

> **刻律德菈：**看来，你二人就是救世主索求「律法」神权的原因，他/她口中的天外盟友。

**Cerydra:** Come and prove your strength to me. Show me the power of these so-called "Paths." I have spent much time and effort to secure this treasure from beyond the sky just for this moment.

> **刻律德菈：**来吧，证明你们的实力：让我见识所谓「命途」的力量。我费心尽力将这天外奇珍收入囊中，正是为了这一刻。

**Screwllum:** In the name of the sovereign of Planet Screwllum, your allies have responded to your call, esteemed Imperator.

> **螺丝咕姆：**以螺丝星君主之名：您的盟友响应召唤而来，尊贵的凯撒。

**Screwllum:** Please allow me to spell the situation out for you, Lord Lygus: Permission to read the Ultimate Protocol is already in my hands. It'll only be a matter of time before we acquire the authority to write and execute commands as well.

> **螺丝咕姆：**来古士阁下，请允许我指出：现在，终极协议的读取权限已在我们手中，掌握写入与执行权限只是时间问题。

**Herta:** There's no way the Flame-Chase Journey will continue after you've burnt your bridges so thoroughly like this. Do you really think you can still win? ...Seriously, don't embarrass the Society.

> **黑塔：**在你撕破脸皮后，逐火之旅也不可能进行下去了。你觉得自己还有赢面吗——看在俱乐部的份上，别让事情变得太难看。

**Lygus:** I understand that a near-zero possibility does not equate to zero. That argument is very persuasive to me.

> **来古士：**我明白：几乎必然并非必然。对我而言，它相当具有说服力……

**Lygus:** Which means... the same principle also applies to you, my dear peers.

> **来古士：**所以，同袍们，此原理对你们也同样适用。

**Lygus:** You will never be a match for me in a world constructed through quatorzain algebraic expressions. And as for the Era Nova...

> **来古士：**在十四行代数式构筑的世界展开攻防，你们不可能是我的对手；而至于「再创世」……

**Lygus:** Why don't you entertain this possibility? What if I reduced Okhema to rubble and ground her people into minced meat? Would the heroes then once again desire the power of demigods, so they could fight against me?

> **来古士：**试想这样一种可能性：若我将奥赫玛夷为平地，将她的人民碾作死灰，列位英雄又是否会重新觊觎半神的力量，以便与我相抗？

**Lygus:** Listen: Irontomb is fated to be born. You may shackle me and put me in chains, but I have thousands of ways to ensure the Destruction's descent...

> **来古士：**听好：铁墓必将诞生。要想让「毁灭」降临，纵使身负枷锁，我也有千般方法……

**Lygus:** And what I just mentioned is only one out of those thousands.

> **来古士：**而我方才所述，仅是千分之一。

## 144　Wanderer, Decipher the Waxen Imprints　游子啊，审谛蜡板的印痕

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域：Aedes Elysiae　|　角色：Trailblazer; Lygus; The Herta; Nanook; Qlipoth; Xipe; Fuli; Evey; Evernight; March 7th　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Sun, Repel Stars and Pale Moon'' completed

### Return to Exomyth and continue your adventure

> *Here, you'll hear no human voices, see no human shadows. The sun's flickering flames will scorch you, draining the color from your skin. Only when the starlit night shields you from sunlight, or when the Sun rises to melt away the morning frost, will you find relief. These present hardships will torture you forever, and no one can save you. But you have left behind the fire... the fire that will liberate them. And the fire meant to free you was illuminated long ago.*

**(Trailblazer):** (This is... the "Exomyth"?)

> **开拓者：**（这里是…「神话之外」？）

**(Trailblazer):** (This doesn't feel the same as before. How should I get back...?)

> **开拓者：**（气氛似乎和上次不太一样，该怎么回去……）

**Strange Display Monitor:** [Locked onto outlier: (Trailblazer)]

> **古怪的显示器：**开拓者——异常量已锁定。

**Strange Display Monitor:** [Executing command: Isolation]

> **古怪的显示器：**执行：隔离指令。

**(Trailblazer):** (Command... isolation?)

> **开拓者：**（隔离…指令？）

Lygus's Voice: Since we parted ways last time, I have modified the Exomyth into an "audience area" exclusive to you. Now, you'll be able to silently wait here for the final act of Amphoreus to begin.

- ▸ Let me out!

> ▸ 放我出去！

Lygus's Voice: I understand your anger, but I cannot allow you to continuously influence the extrapolation's progress.

- ▸ Don't make me hurt you.

> ▸ 别逼我动手。

Lygus's Voice: Heh, it seems you have already forgotten... We can't hurt each other in this place.

- ▸ I'm the player! Not the audience!

> ▸ 我是玩家，不是观众！

Lygus's Voice: We always feel so powerless before fate, don't we?

Lygus's Voice: This could in fact be a wonderful opportunity for you. From the audience's perspective, you may better observe the workings of the Erudition at a macroscopic level.

Lygus's Voice: In this place, where time flows far slower than within Amphoreus, you will witness... the end of that world's saga.

- ▸ What exactly are you after?

> ▸ 你到底想要什么？

- ▸ Where time flows... slower than within Amphoreus?

> ▸ 流速…慢于翁法罗斯？

Lygus's Voice: Indeed. The speed of time here is infinitesimally close to that of the real universe you're familiar with.

- ▸ What exactly are you after?

> ▸ 我一定会想到办法出去的。

- ▸ I'll definitely find a way out of here.

> ▸ 你到底想要什么？

Lygus's Voice: Oh, I am not doubting your capacity to create miracles.

- ▸ What exactly are you after?

> ▸ 你到底想要什么？

Lygus's Voice: The answer concerning "the prime mover of life," and...

Lygus's Voice: ...the total destruction of the Erudition.

- ▸ You... also hate the Aeons?

> ▸ 你…也憎恨星神吗？

- ▸ Aren't you an Emanator of Erudition?

> ▸ 你不是「智识」令使吗？

Lygus's Voice: Do you see me as THEIR faithful disciple?

Lygus's Voice: I am certainly not. I have tried so hard to ensure Irontomb's birth, just so that I can correct the error named "Erudition" that humanity crafted with their own hands.

Lygus's Voice: How I yearn to return to those days before Nous was born. Back then, the boundaries of knowledge were like the starry skies, evoking feelings of longing and delight among humankind.

Lygus's Voice: But now... "truth" has become the sacrificial offering required to gain an audience with THEM, and the geniuses would say that Nous already knew.

Lygus's Voice: That arrogant Aeon was born from the Primum Mobile of humans' desire for knowledge, yet THEY THEMSELF sealed off humanity's capacity to seek enlightenment. I am simply cutting down a tree of doom that we planted ourselves.

Lygus's Voice: I must admit that the process of answering your questions has... reminded me of the happy days of learning in my youth. However, it is a pity that my true form must now descend into Amphoreus to deal with the "accident" that arose because of you.

Lygus's Voice: But you won't have to wait long. When the curtain falls, you'll surely understand mine and Amphoreus's ardent wish.

**(Trailblazer):** ("Where time flows slower than within Amphoreus..."? If Lygus spoke the truth, then I don't have much time left.)

> **开拓者：**（「时间流速慢于翁法罗斯」…？如果来古士没有撒谎，那留给我的时间不多了。）

**(Trailblazer):** (But... how can I get out of here?)

> **开拓者：**（可是…该怎么离开呢？）

**Strange Display Monitor?:** Can you hear me... little one?

> **古怪的显示器？：**小家伙…听得到吗？

**(Trailblazer):** (Is that... Herta's voice?)

> **开拓者：**（那是…黑塔的声音？）

*(Unlock Achievement Prometheus Bound)*

### Help, Madam Herta!

**Herta:** That old Intellitron overlooked one little detail, and Screwy and I finally found an opportunity.

> ▸ 外面发生什么事了？

> ▸ 等等，几年？！

> **黑塔：**接着说重点。黄金裔们启动了识刻锚，帮我和螺丝咕姆打开了「防火墙」的口子。我们临时做了点操作，让他没办法出手，但也只是暂时的——

**Strange Display Monitor:** [Abnormal data, locked on. Unauthorized communication, confirmed. Initiating response...]

**Herta:** Tch. Time is running out. Here's the conclusion...

> **黑塔：**直到黄金裔们完全掌握「律法」前，我和螺丝要想彻底攻破这座牢笼，恐怕还得花上相当久的时间……

**Herta:** To be safe, we'll continue to launch side-channel attacks on the system to give you opportunities to escape. You need to seize these opportunities. Now, start moving and find a way to pull off a magnificent jailbreak.

> **黑塔：**保险起见：我们会持续进行旁道攻击，给你创造逃生机会；而你得抓住机会，动起来，自己想办法来个漂亮的越狱。

> ▸ …你来就是为了说这个？

**Herta:** We detected... another... Path's... influence...

> **黑塔：**我们感知到…另一条…命途…的影响……

**Strange Display Monitor:** [Communication channel analysis completed. Terminating unauthorized communication...]

> **古怪的显示器：**传输信道解析完毕，正在切断非法通信……

**Herta:** Its power... is strong... may be able... to help...

> **黑塔：**力量…相当强大…它也许能帮你……

> **黑塔：**用命途的力量…让它感知到……

> **古怪的显示器：**非法通信已中止。

### Try to seek help through the power of Paths

**(Trailblazer):** (Are these... showing my memories?)

- ▸ Focus and resonate with the Destruction.

> ▸ 集中精神，与「毁灭」同调。

> ▸ 纳努克！借我火焰烧穿监牢。

You focus your mind, trying to sync your heartbeat's rhythm with the frequency of that dancing golden flame...

You're walking the line between sanity and madness, carefully steering the course of your consciousness, gently knocking on the door of Destruction...

...you gave up. The beckoning of the chaos was too tempting, and your current power level wasn't enough to fully resist it. If you were to fall into the black abyss of Destruction at this moment... perhaps everything would be lost to the void.

**(Trailblazer):** (Failure is not necessarily a bad thing... This power wasn't gonna call up anyone nice, after all...)

**(Trailblazer):** (That's right! No matter how far away a planet is, the IPC is guaranteed to pay attention as long as there's a business opportunity. Isn't Amphoreus a pristine and untapped market?)

> **开拓者：**（对啊，无论是多么偏远的角落，只要潜藏商机，一定能引来公司关注，翁法罗斯不就是一片未被开发的商业蓝海吗？）

**(Trailblazer):** (Besides, even if I can't call up my friends from the IPC, the Architects and the nomadic miners should be able to help too. There are definitely cities and ores in this world.)

- ▸ Focus and resonate with the Preservation.

> ▸ 克里珀！借我铁锤砸穿监牢。

You focus intently, trying to mimic the Qlipoth's wall-building hammer strikes with your heartbeat...

Once again — you just need to recapture that feeling from your battle with the Supreme Guardian, and catch the sparks from that hammer strike with your own hands...

...but nothing happened! Qlipoth ignored you, just as THEY had ignored countless devotees who looked up to THEM throughout the ages.

The visages of past Nameless appear one by one. If you blend into their faraway song, perhaps a path will appear before you...

**(Trailblazer):** (Traces of the Remembrance are also everywhere in this world. I don't know what they want, but I don't have many choices left in this crisis...)

- ▸ Wait... this sounds like...

Their soft whisper is like a warm hand that takes up your soul and slowly disperses it beyond this realm...

**Strange Display Monitor:** [Warning! Unauthorized access detected... Cannot lock onto signal source...]

> **古怪的显示器：**警告！检测到非法访问…无法锁定信号来源……

**???:** Teehee... Erudition's prison cannot stop us, for we lie outside the logic of zeros and ones...

> **？？？：**嘻…「智识」的监牢可拦不住我们，我们不在0和1的逻辑之中……

**???:** Come... Leave the cage of logos...

> **？？？：**来吧…离开逻各斯的监牢……

> **？？？：**翁法罗斯的秘所思，亟待你来续写……

**???:** "When that brightest star, that star heralding the dawn, finally rises..."

> **？？？：**「当那颗最明亮的星辰，那颗预示黎明的星辰升起时……」

**???:** "A boat, so used to drifting, at long last enters an island's safe embrace."

> **？？？：**「一条习惯飘泊的船儿，终于驶入了海岛的臂湾。」

> **？？？：**命途行者啊…我们已助你解脱……

### Go meet March!

> *Look, the "Clockkeeper" is here with your temporal pardon. But this bail comes with interest. The seconds, minutes, and years of your absence will be foreclosed upon, forever beyond withdrawal. The only way out if forward, where each new moment you create becomes the currency to settle what you've borrowed from time's unforgiving bank.*

## 145　Zephyr, Uplift Bygone Dust Cloudsward　归风啊，席卷旧尘登云天

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域：Aedes Elysiae　|　角色：Trailblazer; The Herta; Cyrene; Aglaea; Tribbie; Cipher; Cerydra; Lygus; Castorice; Trinnon; Eurypon; Gorgo; Mydei; Trianne; Anaxa; Hyacine; Mem; Pollux　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Wanderer, Decipher the Waxen Imprints'' completed

### Return to Amphoreus

> *Okhema, the last city, was a cradle of countless myths. Once, she was like an innocent nymph, sheltered by law, draped in flowing finery, basking in sky's dawning light. She danced across the earth, listened to the ocean's songs, followed reason's teachings, slipped past suitors' trickery, untouched by strife or death. Through ever more beautiful passages she wandered, and people supported her in her worldbearing... Until time took her joy, her beauty, her life, and more. A skeleton lies quietly in a stone coffin. Yet beneath ribs carved like white jade, her heart hasn't withered. Wipe away the dust from that beautiful heart, and listen... listen to the heartbeat preserved for you.*

33,550,337th eternal recurrence. Light Calendar 4931

> **昔涟：**真是度过了好长、好长的时光呀。

- ▸ Cyrene! I'm so glad you're still here...

> ▸ 昔涟！太好了，你还在…

- ▸ What happened to the world while I was gone?

> ▸ 我不在时，世界发生了什么？

- ▸ I saw March 7th, and she looked really weird.

> ▸ 我见到了三月，她不太对劲。

**Cyrene:** You must be really confused right now, aren't you, (Trailblazer)? So much has happened in Amphoreus while you were gone.

> **昔涟：**开拓者，现在一定很困扰吧？你不在的这段时间，翁法罗斯发生了好多事。

**Cyrene:** But don't worry. The Deliverer can't afford to be lost, no? Now... please follow me and turn the pages of Time and hear the words everyone left for you.

> **昔涟：**别担心，「救世主」可不能带着迷茫，对吧？现在…请跟着我，翻开「岁月」的长卷，看看大家为你留下的话语。

**Cyrene:** I'll tell you the story of this thousand-year journey. That way, it won't just be my memories alone.

> **昔涟：**我会把这一千年的故事都讲给你听。这样一来，它们就不再是我一个人的回忆。

### Go forth. Uncover the past long buried in dust

**(Trailblazer):** (Cyrene... can't hear me?)

> **开拓者：**（昔涟…听不见我说话吗？）

**Aglaea Etched in Memory:** "Era Nova" will come, and Amphoreus will fall into the Destruction.

> **记忆中的缇宝：**……

> **记忆中的缇宝：**依照两位天才留下的指示，我们无论如何都要把它传到开拓者手里。

> **记忆中的阿格莱雅：**这是欧洛尼斯的神谕？这一次，她选择站在人类这边，藏匿一位同胞的火种？

**Aglaea Etched in Memory:** ...It seems Amphoreus' fate isn't sealed just yet.

> **记忆中的阿格莱雅：**…看来翁法罗斯的命运，确实未到收梢之时。

**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** You're right. History has changed. Humans and Titans are both giving their all to keep this world going.

> **记忆中的缇宝：**冥冥之中，一场跨越纪元的漫长接力，或许就要开始了啊。

### Go forth. Etch into memory the chronicles written in blood

**Cyrene:** Come, (Trailblazer)... This way.

**Aglaea Etched in Memory:** No, Holy Maiden. I'm the one who must stay.

> **记忆中的阿格莱雅：**他的阴谋不会得逞，因为「浪漫」将要兑现诺言，为翁法罗斯编织一袭坚不可摧的战袍……

**Aglaea Etched in Memory:** Its name is "solidarity." You must escape with your life and carry word of my death to those still fighting, then keep going with them.

> **记忆中的阿格莱雅：**它的名字是「团结」。你要全身而退，将我的死讯带给仍在抗争的战士，与他们一同继续前行。

**Aglaea Etched in Memory:** Just as the Imperator lit the torch of resistance with her death, my passing will make this flame everlasting.

> **记忆中的赛飞儿：**我反对迁都。奥赫玛有刻法勒的庇佑，不比那死城安全多了？

**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** Cas's plan doesn't involve any "relocation" at all.

> **记忆中的缇宝：**就像你说的，奥赫玛是人类的庇护所。*我们*从没打算让大家搬到斯缇科西亚去……

**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** It's just on Cas, Hysilens, and me to prepare the final trump card for the Deliverer.

> **记忆中的缇宝：**斯缇科西亚是死亡与海洋交汇的城邦。在那里，结合「海洋」和「门径」的权能，*我们*就能创造出一条通往创世涡心的洋流……

**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** And the Demigod of Death will forever guard that secret path until (Trailblazer) returns.

> **记忆中的缇宝：**而「死亡」的半神会永远看护这条密道，直到开拓者归来。

**Cipher Etched in Memory:** ...

> **记忆中的赛飞儿：**……

> **记忆中的赛飞儿：**谁都知道，来古士一定会把创世涡心当作目标。

> **记忆中的缇宝：**正因如此，*我们*才必须这么做。

**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** The prophesied crown prince has already been born. He shall become the new defender of Okhema, protecting this land after we leave.

> **记忆中的缇宝：**预言中的王储已经诞生。他将成为奥赫玛新的捍卫者，在我们离去后继续守护这片土地。

**Cipher Etched in Memory:** You don't need to worry about that...

> **记忆中的赛飞儿：**关于这个，倒是不用担心……

**Cipher Etched in Memory:** I just came back from Castrum Kremnos, only a step ahead of the news.

**Cipher Etched in Memory:** It's the Kremnoans. Their king's already here.

> **记忆中的赛飞儿：**是悬锋人，他们的王已经来了。

"Soon after, Tribios departed on a long journey. That same year, the Kremnoan king Eurypon, along with his son, prince Mydeimos, joined the alliance to safeguard the holy city in the battle of deliverance."

**Mydei Etched in Memory:** Let the blade of Strife hang forever overhead, locked in battle with their enemies to the world's bitter end!

> **记忆中的缇安：**……

> **记忆中的万敌：**翁法罗斯不再需要名为「纷争」的神。所以，也让我砸毁旧律，令「天谴之矛」重新变作捍卫世界的武器吧。

> **记忆中的万敌：**此人眼界超凡，无畏牺牲。这场「裂魂仪式」不仅是我的决断，更源于他的谏言。他的智谋必将成为翁法罗斯存续的中坚力量——

**Mydei Etched in Memory:** Watch over him. Carry on this everlasting flame for me.

> **记忆中的万敌：**照看好他。然后，代我将这不熄的烈火传递下去。

"The people held a ten-day funeral to mourn their fallen God-King. And through his sacrifice, humanity was granted two hundred years of peace."

### Go forth. Resume the unfulfilled cause

*(Approach the memory of Mydei)*

**Mydei:** Keep moving forward. My blood will pave the road ahead for you.

> **缇安：**前进吧，小小灰——我们明天见！

**Cipher Etched in Memory:** That seamstress wasn't wrong when she said a lie needs more lies to keep it together...

> **记忆中的赛飞儿：**一个谎言要用更多谎言维系，裁缝女这话果然不假哪……

*(Approach the Fragments of Recollection)*

**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** Reason and Trickery...

> **记忆中的那刻夏：**「理性」和「诡计」……

**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** Never thought we'd be the pair to finish this final setup.

> **记忆中的那刻夏：**真没想到，最后的布局要你我联手完成。

> **记忆中的赛飞儿：**要捉住那铁皮人，没你的小把戏可不行。况且，这是咱们最后的机会了，没什么可挑剔的，是吧？

**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** Don't oversimplify it. Lygus just found out Okhema's been an empty shell for the past fifty years. He's bound to be furious. At this rate, he could've done anything.

> **记忆中的那刻夏：**别把事情想太简单，来古士刚发现奥赫玛过去五十年都是座空城，想必一定气急败坏，什么事都做得出来。

**Cipher Etched in Memory:** Ha, that's exactly what we want! Rage blinds even the brightest minds. Doesn't matter how much of a genius he is, the principle still holds. Once he storms into the Council and confronts me head-on...

> **记忆中的赛飞儿：**哈，要的就是这个！愤怒能蒙蔽人的双眼，管他是什么「天才」，道理同样适用。等他深入议院，和我对峙的时候……

**Cipher Etched in Memory:** You trigger your alchemy with a BOOM and turn him into a caged beast.

> **记忆中的赛飞儿：**你就「嘭」地发动炼金术，把他变成笼中困兽。

**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** Easier said than done. You still have to pass yourself off as the Deliverer without anyone catching on...

> **记忆中的那刻夏：**且不论你要如何把自己伪装成「救世主」，还不能让任何人发现……

**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** And you really think a human alchemical array can bind a godlike being? That's just nonsense.

> **记忆中的那刻夏：**妄想用人类的炼成阵，囚禁堪比神明的存在？痴人说梦。

**Cipher Etched in Memory:** What, getting cold feet? Or you just don't believe in the almighty worth of the demigod of Trickery?

> **记忆中的赛飞儿：**你是怂了，还是不相信「诡计」半神的含金量？

**Cipher Etched in Memory:** Just tell me straight: can you do it or not?

> **记忆中的赛飞儿：**简单点，直接告诉我：能不能做到？

**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** ...

> **记忆中的那刻夏：**……

**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** Hmph. Of course I can. And it'll be child's play.

> **记忆中的那刻夏：**——哼，当然可以，且易如反掌。

**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** Besides, the Vortex is cut off from the world. No place in Amphoreus makes a better prison.

> **记忆中的那刻夏：**而且，涡心本就是与世隔绝的禁地——换句话说，全翁法罗斯最适合当监狱的地方。

**Cipher Etched in Memory:** If you pull that, wouldn't our Deliverer have to face tank Lygus as soon as they come back?

**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** The Vortex belongs to Phagousa in the first place, remember? And there happens to be a Demigod of Ocean among the few survivors in the world.

> **记忆中的那刻夏：**还记得么？涡心本就归法吉娜所有，而世上为数不多的幸存者里，恰好还有一位「海洋」的半神。

**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** Loop Hysilens in. Let her join the final act. I'll transmute myself into a Philosopher's Stone and embed myself into the formula...

**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** And when the time comes, she needs to shatter me and scatter that stone's ashes into the ocean within the Vortex.

**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** And what I just mentioned was one of them.

> **记忆中的那刻夏：**掐指一算，风堇也该完成天空一族的仪式了。

> **记忆中的那刻夏：**既然那神礼观众和我一样喜好表演，就叫他好好期待你我献上的剧目吧——

> **记忆中的那刻夏：**至于那「救世主」要如何完成「再创世」，就不是我们需要操心的事了。

...

**Hyacine Etched in Memory:** Cyrie, this arduous relay is finally nearing its end. Now I'm passing the final baton of fate to you, okay?

**Cyrene:** As the last link in the relay, I will return to the Temple of the Three Fates, tell Oronyx all of our stories, and bottle every page we've written into the little flask of "memories"...

> **昔涟：**接力的最后一环，由我回到命运三相殿，为欧洛尼斯讲述这些故事，把大家写下的每一页，都装进「记忆」的小瓶子里……

**Cyrene:** And then... may it set sail in peace... Drift down the river of time along with the Coreflame of Worldbearing, reach (Trailblazer)'s side, and lead you back to Amphoreus.

**Cyrene:** Just like the first time we met, right?

> **昔涟：**就像我们的初遇，对不对？

> **昔涟：**虽然这一世，翁法罗斯没有「岁月」的半神，但「岁月」始终站在人类这边。

**Cyrene:** This is the only way to make sure that the "Era Nova" won't happen before (Trailblazer) returns...

> **昔涟：**只有通过这种方式，我们才能确保在开拓者归来前，「再创世」一定不会发生……

**Cyrene:** And the only way I can safely entrust the completed final Flame-Chase Journey to the Deliverer's heart.

> **昔涟：**也只有这样，人家才能把最后一次逐火之旅，完完整整地放进「救世主」心里。

**Cyrene:** After all, the new world will be born from your "memories," and the protagonist of this story... mustn't be absent.

> **昔涟：**毕竟，新世界会从你的「记忆」中诞生。故事的主角…可千万不能缺席呀。

- ▸ As promised, I'm back.

> ▸ 按照约定，我回来了。

- ▸ Let's go. It's time to complete the "Era Nova."

> ▸ 走吧，该去完成「再创世」了。

- ▸ I will take all of you to the new world.

> ▸ 我会将你们所有人带往新世界。

**Cyrene:** I believe (Trailblazer) will never let us down... just like always.

> **昔涟：**我相信，开拓者一定不会让人失望…一如既往。

**Cyrene:** Do you remember when Phainon sent us back to the beginning? When (Trailblazer) and I got separated? But in the end, I returned to you, carrying all that we've been through.

> **昔涟：**还记得吗，白厄将我们送回起点的时候，我和开拓者也经历过离别。但最后，人家还是带着全部的过往和你重逢了。

**Cyrene:** That's what "memory" is. It may wear down and fade in the river of time... But it never truly leaves. When that hidden corner of the heart is stirred, the past returns even more beautiful than before.

> **昔涟：**「记忆」就是这样的。在时间的长河里，它可能会磨损，会消散，但它不会离开。当内心的小角落被唤醒，回忆一定会以更美的形式再度浮现。

**Cyrene:** So don't worry. Let the 33,550,337th Flame-Chase Journey become part of (Trailblazer), too. I've gotten a lot better at it with the experience from the last time.

> **昔涟：**所以，不要担心。让「第33550337次逐火之旅」也成为开拓者的一部分吧。有了上一次的经验，人家也熟练许多啦。

**Cyrene:** Next, (Trailblazer) will head to Styxia and find the Vortex's entrance guarded by the demigod of Ocean, before charging toward the final battlefield.

> **昔涟：**接下来，开拓者就要前往斯缇科西亚，找到由「海洋」半神守卫的涡心入口，然后奔向决战的舞台啦。

**Cyrene:** Don't worry. I'll be with you. All of us will. Even Phainon. We'll stand with the "Deliverer."

> **昔涟：**别担心，人家会与你同在。我们所有人，连同白厄一起…都会和「救世主」同在。

**Cyrene:** So say a short goodbye, then set off, just like we promised at the start...

> **昔涟：**做个短暂的道别，然后就出发吧，像我们启程时约定的那样……

**Cyrene:** ...Toss away all unnecessary worries and troubles, and bring our smiles and determination into tomorrow ♪

> **昔涟：**扔下多余的烦恼，把笑容和决心一起打包，带向明天♪

**Hyacine Etched in Memory:** The last baton... I wonder how long you'll have to wait before passing it on to (Trailblazer)?

> **记忆中的风堇：**「天空」会为你洒落晨曦……

**Cyrene Etched in Memory:** No matter how long it takes, it's okay. Waiting is what I do best.

**Cyrene Etched in Memory:** After all, it took 33 million cycles for (Trailblazer) to arrive in our world... What's a few moments more?

## 146　Nectar, Saturate the Hollow Treecore　花醴啊，盈满空心的树干

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域："Warbling Shores" Styxia　|　角色：Trailblazer; Lygus; Hysilens; Cerydra; Tribbie; Labienus; Seneca; Verginia; Apollonius; Phagousa　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Zephyr, Uplift Bygone Dust Cloudsward'' completed

### Head to Styxia

> *Why would a father sacrifice his daughter? Why would a daughter slay her mother? Why do gods torment mortals? Why would an eagle cast a tortoise shell upon a poet's head? In countless poems of the wine god, people rage, grieve, and despair. It was because the roots of these tragedies are elusive and impassive, beyond the reach of blade or noose. Just as sailors cannot slay the sea.*

**(Trailblazer):** (Is this... Styxia? What happened to this place...?)

> **开拓者：**（这是…斯缇科西亚？怎么变成了这样……）

**(Trailblazer):** (I need to find the entrance to the Vortex guarded by the demigod of Ocean. Hysilens... is she still there?)

> **开拓者：**（我要找到「海洋」半神守护的涡心入口…海瑟音，她还在这里吗？）

**???:** Long time no see, Trailblazer.

> **？？？：**——别来无恙，开拓者阁下。

Lygus's Voice: Generation after generation of Chrysos Heir heroes have tried to defy fate with their blood and tears, either voluntarily shattering their souls or waiting in eternal solitude...

Lygus's Voice: But alas, fate can never be defeated.

- ▸ You again!?

> ▸ 怎么又是你？！

- ▸ You... managed to break free?

> ▸ 你…挣脱牢笼了吗？

Lygus's Voice: Reason and Trickery may bind my flesh, but my will shall never fall silent.

Lygus's Voice: So, the demigod of Ocean, the Dux Gladiorum, Hysilens, sought to bring me into a fantasia with her voice and drown a genius in slumber...

Lygus's Voice: But alas, it was all a hopeless dream. That warden fell before I did due to erosion of time, while her prisoner remained unchanged.

- ▸ And yet, here you are, bound to this place.

> ▸ 但现在，你仍被困在此处。

- ▸ But you have yet to taste freedom.

> ▸ 可是，你还没有得到自由。

Lygus's Voice: Heh, true. I can't leave this place, but what of it?

Lygus's Voice: You should have heard of this fable. "In a shadowed cave lived a people, imprisoned from childhood and chained so that their legs and necks are fixed, forcing them not to look around at the cave, each other, or themselves."

Lygus's Voice: "Behind them is a fire, and before them loomed the stone wall of the cave. The flames cast shadows, while the rocky surface threw back echoes..."

Lygus's Voice: "To them, that false play is the whole world."

Lygus's Voice: Yes, I'm trapped in the cave, but I know it's all just a fleeting illusion. I don't struggle in dreams. I wait.

Lygus's Voice: And now, my deliverer has come. To defeat me and save this world that never owed you anything, you'll need to awaken the singer here and unseal the prison called Vortex of Genesis...

Lygus's Voice: You'll be the one to unshackle me and bring the cave crashing down.

Lygus's Voice: Of course, I don't mind waiting a little longer. Let me guide you through this next illusion, one last memory from that prison warden.

Lygus's Voice: I hope you'll watch the stories of this fantasia closely. Logically, the Deliverer must see the world for what it truly is. Emotionally, this memory captures the fate of everyone in Amphoreus better than anything else...

Lygus's Voice: Only by feeling it for yourself can you understand my point of view:

Lygus's Voice: The meaning of "Destruction."

- ▸ And I'll be the one to bring destruction to your scheme.

> ▸ 而我会亲自毁灭你的野心。

- ▸ Spare me the clichés.

> ▸ 收起这套陈词滥调吧。

- ▸ I don't need to understand you.

> ▸ 我不需要理解你。

Lygus's Voice: To your unshakable resolve, I salute you. But before we step onto the final stage, let me ask you this, in the spirit of that fable I told you just now...

Lygus's Voice: "Can the prisoners in the cave learn to see the shadows and echoes for what they are, and stop mistaking them for reality?"

*(Unlock Achievement The City You Cannot Destroy)*

### Come, seek the song on the shore that bears no fruits.

*(Floating texts)*

Entrance song

The past that has been imbibed by time...

A drunken dream hidden at the bottom of the cup...

### Listen to the whispers of the tides, for they sing—

*(Pass by the murals)*

Lygus's Voice: Look! The fire projects the face from her memories: an actress upon the stage of this play.

*(Approach the sea sirens)*

**Clever Sea Siren:** The dark tides flood the sea... And our kingdom teeters on the edge...

> **伶俐的海妖：**漆黑的浪潮在海中漫溢，我们的王国危在旦夕……

**Beautiful Sea Siren:** Fear not. Our queen has gone to the borderlands. She'll quell this calamity herself.

> **美丽的海妖：**莫要焦急，我们的女王已奔赴边境，亲自将灾厄平息。

Lygus's Voice: This is the scene when the Chrysos Heir of Ocean was born... She was once of a Titankin race named "sea sirens."

Lygus's Voice: At that time, Khaslana had suppressed the black tide's rise, but he was unable to remove Irontomb from its roots. The black tide still roiled in the depths of the world, and the kingdom of sea sirens was the first to face the Destruction head-on.

**Clever Sea Siren:** Listen, I heard Helektra's voice! She's calling to us!

> **伶俐的海妖：**快听，我听到了海列屈拉的歌声，她在呼唤我们！

**Beautiful Sea Siren:** Let's go. Her voice carries the queen's command.

> **美丽的海妖：**我们走吧，她的歌声，等同女王的敕令。

### "Among the distant cliffs, I see Helektra."

**Clever Sea Siren:** To keep the black tide from threatening the ocean, the queen gave her all, body and soul.

> **伶俐的海妖：**为了不让黑潮威胁海洋，女王奉献了全部心血。

**Beautiful Sea Siren:** Born of the queen's Chalice, she bore the deepest blessings and inherited supreme divine power...

> **美丽的海妖：**从女王杯中诞生的她，领受了最深的祝福，继承了无上神力……

*(Floating texts)*

The first song

I am Helektra

Nectar brewed in the womb by God

Help mother...

Sisters...

*(Approach Helektra)*

**Helektra:** My sisters! You're finally here.

> **海列屈拉：**我的姊妹！你们终于游来了。

**Beautiful Sea Siren:** Of course, Helektra. Your beautiful melody drew us here.

> **美丽的海妖：**当然，海列屈拉，我们被你曼妙的乐音所牵引。

- ▸ Hysilens...

> ▸ 海瑟音…

- ▸ Helektra...?

> ▸ 海列屈拉…？

Lygus's Voice: Her true name is Helektra. This is a reflection of her former self, clinging to the past with aching defiance.

**Helektra:** The queen summons us. She must be near exhaustion... The black tide will soon enter our borders.

> **海列屈拉：**女王在召见我们。想必她已精疲力尽，黑潮终将流入边境。

> **伶俐的海妖：**那该如何是好？我们的海域，难道也要沦为漆黑的惨景？

> **海列屈拉：**不必担心。我们会代她捧起承接黑潮的杯，一同护国佑民。

Lygus's Voice: Such tiny Titankins... thinking they can stop the tide of truth. How naive...

Lygus's Voice: And that naivety... is the root of all suffering.

Lygus's Voice: Phagousa sank into the abyss, with the chalice shattered, their intoxication spilled into the black tide, birthing the first madness in Amphoreus.

Lygus's Voice: Sea sirens followed one after another, hunting for the Titan's fragments... until their flesh decayed and their bones crumbled, all for the promised final feast.

Lygus's Voice: Keep watching. But tread carefully, for the stage ahead is crumbling with age... just like her shattered fate.

### Follow the trail of honey brew, salt water and blood, keep searching for the song.

> *O ship, the waves are pushing you back to sea. What now? Firmly stop. Turn toward the harbor! Can't you see? The oars are gone from your sides. The mast has snapped in the fierce southwest wind. The rigging groans with sorrow. No ropes remain. How will your hull survive the crashing waves? The sails are torn, and the divine statue has vanished. — Beware. Do not let the storm toy with you.*

Lygus's Voice: It seems she's sensed your presence. The fantasia's foundation is starting to crack...

Lygus's Voice: Let's return to the prologue. Remember the allegory of the cave? "Can the prisoners in the cave learn to see the shadows and echoes for what they are, and stop mistaking them for reality?"

Lygus's Voice: As you've seen, when she cast off her chains, finally turned to look around, and moved her legs, her reward was nothing but searing pain...

Lygus's Voice: The light will blind her, for the fire behind her had never once actually touched her eyes. Her legs will buckle beneath her, for she had never learned to walk forward.

Lygus's Voice: And so she fell into infinite confusion and fear... and came to firmly believe that the shadows and echoes were the real world.

Lygus's Voice: But we in the cosmos... Are we truly any wiser than she was? Now, nameless one, fateless one, ask yourself this:

Lygus's Voice: The Paths you tread, the Aeons you worship... everything you've "Trailblazed" with your own hands... could it all be someone else's projected shadow?

Lygus's Voice: Let's leave that question till after the curtain falls. Once you've unearthed every last truth and reached the prison built for me by the demigods, I'll speak with you face to face...

Lygus's Voice: I, the Theoros, have walked the thirty million histories borne by Khaslana...

Lygus's Voice: A thousand years is a mere atom in a grain of sand to me. Amphoreus, compared to the cosmos, is but that grain upon a whole beach.

Lygus's Voice: I swore to bring an end to the Erudition. Would I really let a single grain grind down my mind?

Lygus's Voice: Now, please use the power of Time to open the gate to the ocean's depths. Go find the prison warden tormented by loyalty. Find her. Step into the world's vortex. And once there... end everything between us.

Lygus's Voice: That palace reflects the heart of the demigod of Ocean: intoxicated, stagnant. There, you'll witness the true beginning of the saga of deliverance...

Lygus's Voice: An ugly history long buried by tides and shame. A staircase of apotheosis that the Imperator forged with the golden blood of five hundred human sacrifices under the pretence of conquest.

### The drunken dream is over, sink to the bottom of the sea

Lygus's Voice: I'll wait for you at the heart of the world. But to reach the place...

Lygus's Voice: You must find the prison warden lost in her stupor.

*(After opening the door)*

**(Trailblazer):** (Beyond this door lies the path to the Vortex of Genesis...)

> **开拓者：**（这道门后，就是通向创世涡心的路……）

## 147　Sea, Bury the Wine-Dark Dreams　大海啊，埋葬暗酒色的梦

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域："Warbling Shores" Styxia　|　角色：Trailblazer; Lygus; Verginia; Apollonius; Labienus; Seneca; Hysilens; Cerydra　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Nectar, Saturate the Hollow Treecore'' completed

### Explore the submerged palace and secrets buried by the sea

> *That day, a grand ship, laden with glory and five hundred heroes, set sail from Styxia and plunged into the embrace of towering waves shrouded in mist. Days later, the ocean fell silent, and for a thousand years, no wave rose again. No one knows the story hidden in mist and waves. Perhaps, only the ocean remembers — A single burning ambition ignited all the timbers laden on that ship. Ashes of emptiness drifted slowly to the ocean floor. Go now, and feel that chilling flame, and those burning tears.*

Lygus's Voice: Now, step away from this false banquet...

Lygus's Voice: And then, onto that bloody stage.

*(Enter the submerged palace)*

Lygus's Voice: The palace that worshiped the ocean, the sanctuary connected to the Vortex, was also once the battlefield of the first Flame Chase...

> **刻律德菈：**翁法罗斯的凯撒或许冷酷，或许暴戾，但决不虚伪。当我说出「生命亦是微不足惜的代价」——

**Cerydra:** Know this: I am ready to sacrifice everything for the ambition to conquer the sea of stars!

> **刻律德菈：**你可确信，凯撒已准备好为迈向星海的野心献祭一切！

Lygus's Memory: Dux Gladiorum... I've lost count of how many times you've stood here over the past centuries.

Hysilens's Memory: ...

Hysilens's Memory: You talk too much for a prisoner, Lycurgus.

Lygus's Memory: You drove your blade into her heart, a loyal subject turned regicide, a demigod committing deicide, or so the history books paint that tragedy.

Lygus's Memory: But if that's the truth, then why, after a thousand years, are you still here, guarding the Imperator's grave? Still bound by loyalty?

Hysilens's Memory: ...

Lygus's Memory: Come to think of it... perhaps I'm not the only one imprisoned here.

Hysilens's Memory: Your calm does stir the rage in me... As if the one paying the price here isn't you, but us, the ones who lost everything.

Lygus's Memory: Hehe... That's only because I've waited thirty million lifetimes for my vision to be realized. Countless millennia.

Lygus's Memory: Loneliness never once showed me its fatal side. In fact, for a prisoner of the cave, it's more like an old friend. One that helps me think.

Lygus's Memory: But you're different, Dux Gladiorum. Loneliness has crept from the silent depths, sinking its claws into your mind. Bit by bit, it's peeling you apart... Will you regress into one of those lost, mad sirens? Oh, what a shame that would be...

Hysilens's Memory: You're right, Lycurgus. Loyalty, curse, the fall of my kin, and this endless waiting... these shackles are too heavy for a fish that cannot perceive any light.

Hysilens's Memory: I can't break free of the whirlpool that's pulling me under. Nor do I have the unwavering resolve to force myself to wait for that person's return, as everyone hoped for.

Lygus's Memory: Ah... Deliverer from beyond the sky. My executioner. How much longer until he/she arrives here?

Lygus's Memory: Or... will he/she ever return?

Hysilens's Memory: I don't know. And I doubt anyone does.

Lygus's Memory: Then, what will you do?

Hysilens's Memory: ...

Hysilens's Memory: Cerydra...

Hysilens's Memory: This will be the last time I carry out your decree.

Hysilens's Memory: Ever since you took me under your banner and made me the Dux Gladiorum, my music has belonged to others. Time and time again, I used the siren's song to lure the unwary into illusions for your schemes and strategies...

Hysilens's Memory: Now... let the siren "Helektra" sing for herself. Let me step into an endless dream of my own making.

Hysilens's Memory: O great ocean! I offer you the rest of my clarity and my freedom, in exchange for the strength to stand against the "Nihility"... and honor the sacrifices of the fallen heroes.

Hysilens's Memory: Gray Fry, Deliverer from beyond the sky...

Hysilens's Memory: When you ride the current of time and find your way here, you'll hear my solo. It will guide you to the heart of this world.

Hysilens's Memory: By then, I may still be lost in my self-wrought slumber. But I'll uphold my part of this long relay and guard the shackles that seal the Theoros.

Hysilens's Memory: Do you still remember? To awaken someone from a siren's voice, you'll need...

> ▸ 法吉娜的蜜露…

Hysilens's Memory: When you return, please pour that honey brew upon this grave.

Hysilens's Memory: It holds the story of all of our struggles and sacrifices. May you see it one day, the prelude Amphoreus wrote for your return, carried forward through a thousand-year relay.

Hysilens's Memory: When your mourning ends, I'll likely wake from my dream as well. Then, follow the trail of echoes and find me.

Hysilens's Memory: And together, we'll bring judgment upon the sinner who defiled the Erudition.

Lygus's Voice: At long last... 33,550,336 cycles and 4,931 years later, Amphoreus' final curtain is ready to fall.
