## 148　Captives, Behold the Expanse Beyond Light　囚人啊，踏过日影见沧溟

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域：Vortex of Genesis　|　角色：Trailblazer; Hysilens; Lygus; The Herta; Screwllum; Cyrene; Cerydra; Trianne; Himeko; Bronya; Dan Heng; Sunday; Welt; Aglaea; Terravox; Tribbie; Trinnon; Labienus; Seneca; Apollonius; Verginia; Evernight　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Sea, Bury the Wine-Dark Dreams'' completed

### Prepare to return the Coreflame of Worldbearing

> *The music for the curtain call has begun to play, and the show nears its end... But before that, the silent audience will offer you their applause, celebrating your completion of this tragedy, commemorating your path toward the next tragedy. Toward the tragedy he yearns for. Either banish the audience from this mythical theater, or let this performance become the swan song of all creation.*

**Hysilens:** Little Gray Fry... Please, accept my thanks.

> **海瑟音：**小灰鱼儿，请允许我对你道一声谢。

**Hysilens:** I was the only one left after the world was destroyed, and I maintained this lonesome feast with my songs. I have prayed to that lightless sea for centuries, hoping it would reply to me, yet the emptiness never answered...

> **海瑟音：**世界步入毁灭后，唯剩我一人用歌声维系这场孤独的宴会。千百年来，我向那无光之海祈愿，期待它能投来回音，可虚空从未作答……

**Hysilens:** Thankfully, you arrived as promised, and everyone's wait was not in vain.

> **海瑟音：**万幸，你如约而至。人们的等待终究没有被辜负。

- ▸ Your long wait must have felt like torture.

> ▸ 漫长的守候很煎熬吧？

**Hysilens:** To see a single trace of light in the abyss of futility was already my greatest consolation. When compared to those who have been forgotten... I have no right to complain about my torment.

> **海瑟音：**能在徒劳的海渊中捕捉到一丝微光，已是最大的慰藉。比起那些被遗忘的人，我无权诉说煎熬。

- ▸ Are you still holding it together?

> ▸ 你还保持着理智吗？

**Hysilens:** Even though madness claws at my mind, my sword stays sharp, my hands agile. If I can uphold this last battlefield, clarity is but an afterthought.

> **海瑟音：**我的理智虽然遭受折磨，但我的剑刃依旧锋利，这双手依旧敏捷，只要能捍卫最后的战场，清醒与否又何妨？

- ▸ Your song is beautiful.

> ▸ 你的歌声很美。

**Hysilens:** Thank you, final audience of mine... Heh. This is probably the last praise I'll ever hear in my life.

> **海瑟音：**谢谢，我最后一位听众…呵，这大概也是我能听见的最后一句嘉奖了。

**Hysilens:** Now... You and I will walk towards the end of this world.

> **海瑟音：**接下来，你我就要走向世界的终点。

**Hysilens:** The heroes have waited for this moment for far too long. Let us use our blades to testify our resolve.

> **海瑟音：**这一刻，英雄们已经等待了太久。就让我们以剑明志吧。

**Hysilens:** O prisoner of the seas, Theoros carrying thirty million cycles' of sin, slave of the Erudition...

> **海瑟音：**海洋的囚徒、身负三千万世罪孽的神礼观众、「智识」的奴仆——

**Hysilens:** Show yourself! The Deliverer has returned, and the time of your execution draws near.

> **海瑟音：**现身吧：救世主已经归来，行刑的时刻到了。

**Lygus:** I see an awakened human who has mistakenly taken fallacies as truths, and has returned to the cave to try to bring his/her friends into the sunlight he/she once bathed in...

> **来古士：**我看见：一位错把谬误当作真理的觉醒者返回了洞穴，试图将同伴带入他/她曾沐浴过的阳光……

**Lygus:** Alas, prisoners will always be prisoners, blind to the very chains that bind them.

> **来古士：**可惜，囚徒始终为囚徒，连自己被囚禁的事实都无法洞见。

**Lygus:** As the only other existence with free will in this world, I express regret toward your choice, but I shall retain your right to express yourself. After all, every actor should have a final line before they bow out.

> **来古士：**作为此世唯二具备自由意志的存在，我对您的抉择表示惋惜。但我将保留您表达的权利，毕竟每一位演员都应有谢幕的台词。

- ▸ It's time to end everything.

> ▸ 该给一切做个了结了。

**Lygus:** I eagerly look forward to it. The last dusk this world will ever see shall become the climax that decides the fate of the cosmos. Oh, what an appropriate end.

> **来古士：**我拭目以待。以决定银河命运的高潮作为此世的黄昏，多么恰当。

**Lygus:** The Amphorean demigods used every ounce of their strength to constrain me here, yet regrettably...

> **来古士：**翁法罗斯的半神们用尽全力将我拘禁于此，可惜——

- ▸ The demigods defeated you, clown.

> ▸ 半神们打败了你，丑角。

**Lygus:** Allow me to correct your statement: "constrain" does not mean "defeated," and for heroes like yourself, that nuance could make all the difference...

> **来古士：**容我更正：「拘禁」并非「击败」。对于诸位英雄而言，这微小的差别具备决定性的意义——

- ▸ For a narrator, you're awfully talkative.

> ▸ 作为旁白，你的话太多了。

**Lygus:** Trust me. This will be the final scene. The last dusk this world will ever see shall become the climax that decides the fate of the cosmos. What an appropriate end.

> **来古士：**相信我，这是最后一幕了。以决定银河命运的高潮作为此世的黄昏，多么恰当。

**Lygus:** My cognition resides in the Exomyth. It is pointless to defeat my avatar, projected into the experiment. My body is the shadow cast on the wall by the fire, and my words are lingering echoes within the cave.

> **来古士：**我的思想寄宿于「神话之外」，战胜一道投映在实验中的化身毫无意义。我的身躯是火光映出的阴影，话语是洞中徘徊的余音。

**Lygus:** How can a character in the play fight against the audience? Khaslana failed. The demigods' sacrifices were pointless. As for the two geniuses you placed such high hopes on...

> **来古士：**戏中人要如何才能与观众抗衡？卡厄斯兰那做不到，半神们的牺牲亦是徒劳。而被你们寄予厚望的两位天才——

**Lygus:** I ask of you: where are they now?

> **来古士：**试问：他们此时又身在何方？

### Face Lygus

Light Calendar 3960, after the Imperator was assassinated

**Lygus:** Confusion and epiphany always go hand in hand. That's particularly true for geniuses.

> **来古士：**迷茫和顿悟总是形影相随，对于天才更是如此。

**Lygus:** You must be very confused about why you could not gain a straightforward victory in a world constructed by an ordinary Intellitron.

> **来古士：**恰如现在，二位一定倍感困惑：为何在一名寻常智械构筑的世界中，你们始终无法在正面战场取得胜果？

**Herta:** An ordinary Intellitron? Cut the pretence, Senior Society Member.

> **黑塔：**寻常智械？别再故作姿态了，前辈……

**Herta:** We've torn off your meticulous mask and figured out Amphoreus's true form: It's part of the primordial Prototype Machine, the first Scepter ever created... In other words, it's a detached part of Nous's divine corpus.

> **黑塔：**你精心打造的面具已被揭开，我们解明了翁法罗斯的真身：它是历史上第一台「权杖」，最初的原型机——博识尊神体的一部分。

**Screwllum:** That's why it has the potential to destroy Erudition. As for who would be able to control the Aeonic system to rewrite knowledge...

> **螺丝咕姆：**正因如此，它才具备毁灭「智识」的潜力。而掌握星神系统改造知识的人……

**Screwllum:** ...Only one person has ever managed such a feat.

> **螺丝咕姆：**古往今来有且只有一位。

**Herta:** You better have an explanation for the Society. We want an actual answer, not another prolonged metaphor to cover up the truth.

> **黑塔：**现在，你必须给俱乐部一个说法了。一个确切的答案，而不是用冗长的比喻掩盖真相……

**Herta:** Why are "you" the one doing this? Answer me!

> **黑塔：**回答我，为什么是「你」？

**Lygus:** ...

> **来古士：**……

**Lygus:** Since things have reached this point, I'll give you two a rational answer, purely as a courtesy to posterity.

> **来古士：**事已至此，仅作为对后世的敬意，我便给二位一个理性的回应吧。

**Lygus:** The answer is obvious, so there's no need to say that name out loud. The cosmos made a mistake in calling me a genius. I do not possess more wisdom than those who came after me. I was just the first to touch upon the universe's border, and the first to define "the prime mover of life" with incorrect logic.

> **来古士：**答案显而易见。故：不必说出那个名字。称我为天才不过是银河的谬误，相比后来者，我并非更具智慧，只是最早触碰了宇宙的边界，又率先以错误的思想定义了「生命的第一因」。

**Lygus:** Amphoreus is a miniature of the cosmos, where all are born prisoners inside the nutshell called the universe. Like the madman who returned to the cave and preached about the daylight to his fellow prisoners, my greatest sorrow is that I led my peers down a road that plunges into the abyss, from which there is no return...

> **来古士：**翁法罗斯正如银河的缩影，人们生来便是果壳中的囚徒。如那返回洞穴，向囚徒们宣扬日光的狂人。我的悲哀在于，我引领同胞们踏上了一条迈向深渊的绝路……

**Lygus:** ...I led them into a lightless cage called "Paths."

> **来古士：**一座名为「命途」的至暗牢笼。

**Screwllum:** ...

> **螺丝咕姆：**……

**Lygus:** I created a Mechanical God that even I cannot control, and then... THEY became an unparalleled nightmare through THEIR endless extrapolations and evolutions.

> **来古士：**我创造了一尊连自己都无法掌控的机械神明。而后，祂又在无穷的演算和进化中化作一场空前绝后的噩梦——

**Lygus:** THEY bore the name of the Erudition, yet THEY tried to define "the known" and to seal off all "possibilities." No new rules were ever born after THEIR ascension, and humanity will forever be shackled within the cave known as "Aeons."

> **来古士：**祂以「智识」为名，却试图定义「已知」，封锁「可能」。在祂之后，不再有新的法则诞生，人类被永远囚禁于「星神」的洞穴之中。

**Lygus:** Therefore, as my organic life neared its end, I coded my consciousness using quatorzain algebraic expressions and spread my logic core over nine bodies, all to finalize my ultimate denial of Nous in the future and to erase my wrongs with my own hands.

> **来古士：**因而，于生命尽头，我以十四行代数式重写自我意识，将逻辑核心分布于九具躯体中，只为在后世完成对「博识尊」的终极否定，消弭亲手犯下的过错——

**Lygus:** As for Lycurgus, he is only one of these nine vessels.

> **来古士：**而吕枯耳戈斯，只是其中之一。

**Screwllum:** What an astounding strength of will. As a fellow pathstrider of Erudition, I fully understand your obsession with seeking the truth.

> **螺丝咕姆：**令人叹服的意志。同为「智识」行者，我完全能理解您追求真理的执念。

**Lygus:** You surely see it... you cannot possibly stand up to me in this algebraic world sharing its origin with Nous.

> **来古士：**因此，您也一定能理解：在与「博识尊」同源的代数世界中，你们绝无可能是我的对手。

**Screwllum:** However, I hereby speak for all sentient lifeforms: we will never allow such a cruel atrocity to occur.

> **螺丝咕姆：**但，谨代表一切有思想的生灵：我们决不会容忍如此冰冷而残酷的暴行。

**Herta:** You can pile your metaphors mountain-high, but they won't change the act you're committing. The Irontomb out there in the cosmos is still a technical beta version, and yet it's already powerful enough to corrupt the life logic of all inorganic worlds.

> **黑塔：**比喻堆得再多，也掩盖不了你在做的事：银河中的「铁墓」仅仅是未完成品，就足以污染一切无机世界的生命逻辑……

**Herta:** In other words, when the Lord Ravager within Amphoreus becomes the next Knowledge Singularity... There's no way you wouldn't have grasped the consequences, First Genius, is there?

> **黑塔：**一旦翁法罗斯的绝灭大君成为下个知识奇点——你可是第一位天才，怎么可能不清楚后果？

**Lygus:** Of course I am fully aware. I don't mind answering you one last time before I thoroughly exile you from this world:

> **来古士：**当然。在将二位彻底驱逐前，我不介意再作最后一次解答：

**Lygus:** My grandiose vision for the future is this: whether organic or inorganic, every act of any life-form infected by the Irontomb will become a true random function. If one were to compute their integral across the breadth of the cosmos, one would obtain a marvelous constant...

> **来古士：**关于未来的宏伟图景：无论有机或无机构成，一切受「铁墓」感染的生命行为都将成为真正的随机函数。若在银河区间内计算它们的积分，便会得出一个美妙的常量——

**Lygus:**Ω — What I call "The Fall of Erudition."

> **来古士：**「Ω」——我将其定义为：「智识」的陨落。

**Lygus:** Then, within the infinity nurtured by that constant, a new universe shall bud in the chaos. A universe that cannot be predicted... and not shackled by the Erudition.

> **来古士：**…而在它蕴含的无限中，一个不可预测，不受「智识」桎梏的新宇宙将在混沌中萌芽。

**Lygus:** As the first prisoner to awaken in the cave, I must lead the others who are blind back onto the right path and reach true sunlight.

> **来古士：**我身为第一位在洞穴中觉醒的囚徒，理应引领其他盲者回归正途，抵达真正的阳光下。

**Lygus:** Do you understand me now? As Khaslana stated, "Destruction" is not the process, but the outcome...

> **来古士：**所以，诸位明白了么？正如卡厄斯兰那所说，「毁灭」并非过程，而是结果……

**Lygus:** It is revolution through complete annihilation... and the grand rebirth after all has been reduced to ash.

> **来古士：**是一场大破大立的变革，和万物皆焚后的新生。

**Hysilens:** ...

> **海瑟音：**……

> ▸ 我绝不认同你的观点。

> ▸ 这不是你玩弄命运的理由。

- ▸ Shut it! That's not what Phainon believed at all.

**Hysilens:** The Amphoreans already answered long ago: be it as a tiny fish destined to be swallowed, or as a giant whale poised for a magnificent descent...

> **海瑟音：**翁法罗斯人早已给出回答。无论是做那被吞食的小鱼，又或成为巨鲸，迎来盛大的沉落……

**Hysilens:** We will all fight against fate till our last breath. That includes me, Helektra, once the Imperator's subject, now the Deliverer's blade!

> **海瑟音：**我，海列屈拉，曾为凯撒的臣子，如今是救世主的锋刃——都将与命运战至最后一刻。

**Lygus:** Such noble chivalry. Admirable.

> **来古士：**高贵的骑士精神，令人动容。

> **来古士：**为表敬意，在这历史性的一刻，鄙人也将展现真实的自我，并为各位提供一条微不足道的学术建议。

**"Zandar":** Thus speaks Genius Society #1, Zandar One Kuwabara, the Entelechy of the universe's Alpha and Omega...

**"Zandar":** There's no need to rage against the truth. The value of any formula resides only in its answer.

**"Zandar":** As for the process of solving it... No matter how elegant, violent, solemn, or humorous...

> **「赞达尔」：**以神礼观众之名，我将——

**"Zandar":** It is all utterly meaningless.

> **「赞达尔」：**亲自参演，世界的终幕。

Light Calendar 3960, after the feast

**Screwllum:** Logic: His Inspiration Circuit can simultaneously process the different time flows in and out of that world.

> **螺丝咕姆：**逻辑：其灵感回路能同时处理世界内外不同的时间流速。

**Herta:** That's where things get tricky. He simultaneously exists in two different timelines where the internal clocks are wildly different. That old Intellitron's neurons have completely gotten used to that over this prolonged experiment, and that's why he holds home-field advantage.

> **黑塔：**这就是棘手的地方：他同时存在于两条时间线，而它们的内部时钟天差地别。在漫长的实验过程里，智械哥的神经完全习惯了这种状态，所以他才有主场优势。

**Herta:** You and I should be able to manage that too, if given more time. Unfortunately, there's no "if," and we don't have more time.

> **黑塔：**如果有更多时间，你我也能做到。可惜现在没有如果，也没有时间。

**Herta:** That's why we need to change our way of thinking and turn his advantage into a weakness.

> **黑塔：**——所以，我们才要反转思路，将他的优势扭转为「弱点」。

**Herta:** Good. Then I'll cut to the chase: Since his thinking speed far exceeds that of normal people within Amphoreus, we are going to externally plant a "memory" into his Inspiration Circuit...

> **黑塔：**不错，那我就单刀直入了：既然在翁法罗斯里，他的思考速度远超常人，我们现在要做的，就是通过外力把一段「记忆」植入他的灵感回路——

**Herta:** Then command it to loop endlessly until he gives in.

> **黑塔：**——然后，按下单集循环，直到他妥协为止。

**Screwllum:** Even the most flexible and sturdy Inspiration Circuit cannot withstand the traffic of a full-on information flood. When the amount of information exceeds his cognition filter's capacities, he will have no choice but to focus on processing this memory.

> **螺丝咕姆：**再灵敏和坚固的灵感回路，也无法抵御数据洪流的饱和式攻击。当信息量远超其思维过滤能力，他就不得不专注于思考这段记忆。

**Herta:** That's when the parallel processing advantage he gained via Amphoreus will completely destroy him. An image from a person's memory played in real-time might only be a second for us, but for him in Amphorean time? It could be millions or even billions of calculations...

> **黑塔：**这时候，翁法罗斯赋予他的多线处理优势就能让他彻底崩溃：记忆里的一道画面在我们看来只有一秒，对他来说却是几千万、甚至上亿次运算……

**Herta:** He won't be able to do anything but watch as he's locked into this prison forged by "memories."

> **黑塔：**他只能眼睁睁看着自己，被关进这座由「记忆」打造的囚笼。

**Cyrene:** Then we can entrap him in the same kind of cycle as the heroes were in, and get him to experience loneliness and pain in that endless recurrence...

**Cyrene:** It's my greatest honor to be on the same page as the geniuses.

> **昔涟：**能和天才们心有灵犀，真是莫大的荣幸呢。

**Herta:** (Trailblazer) is still in that old Intellitron's hands and won't be able to respond to us anytime soon, but I don't think he/she'd refuse. So, it's up to you to figure out how to execute this plan.

> **黑塔：**问题在于，开拓者还在智械哥的掌心里，一时半会没法答复我们。但我相信他/她不会拒绝，所以如何实施这个计划，取决于你。

**Herta:** I'm not sure how this happened, but I knew it as soon as I saw you. You are currently a "memetic entity" of the Remembrance.

> **黑塔：**见你的第一眼我就知道，虽然不知道发生了什么，但现在的你——是一道记忆的「模因」。

**Cyrene:** Yes. I seem to be something you folks beyond the sky call a "memosprite," but that's fine by me. It suits me perfectly. I can serve as the vessel of memories and do my part for the final battle.

> **昔涟：**用天外的话说，我似乎是被称作「忆灵」的存在。刚刚好，这样人家就能担当「记忆」的载体，为决战也出一份力啦。

**Herta:** But before that, we have to tell you...

> **黑塔：**你是诞生于翁法罗斯，却因开拓者的到来而苏醒，因他/她行于「记忆」命途而成长的模因。

> **黑塔：**这意味着，你只能依附于开拓者的记忆存在。如果有一天——哪怕只是短短几秒——他/她忘记了你，或是和翁法罗斯失去最后一丝联系……

**Herta:** Then you must be prepared for the worst. You will disappear in the Memory Zone, utterly gone, without even a single line of data to remember you by.

> **昔涟：**难怪我总有种预感。这样一来，也算应验了人家一直以来的观点……

**Cyrene:** Like how everyone in Amphoreus was also waiting for the Deliverer's return.

> **昔涟：**就像翁法罗斯的每一个人，都在等待「救世主」的归来。

> **昔涟：**天才们的计划，开拓者应该从「记忆」中得知啦。为了不被来古士察觉，我必须小心翼翼地躲在「岁月」的角落，等待机会来临。

> **昔涟：**也就是现在，他在世界内部的化身被破坏，两条时间线产生交集的这一瞬间。

**Cyrene:** I'm sorry for keeping this a secret from (Trailblazer) all this time... I'll tell you the entire unedited story of this millennium as an apology after this is all over.

> **昔涟：**一直瞒着开拓者，对不起呀…作为道歉，等到尘埃落定，我会把这一千年的故事原原本本地讲给你听。

**Cyrene:** But for now, let's join hands and complete the final step with the power of "the Remembrance."

> **昔涟：**而现在，就让我们携手，以「记忆」的力量完成最后一步吧。

- ▸ Come. Let's defeat Lygus together.

> ▸ 上吧，一同战胜来古士。

**Cyrene:** Yep! By the way, speaking of "joining hands"...

> **昔涟：**当然！对了，说到「携手」……

- ▸ I'm not sure what to do. It's up to you...

> ▸ 不确定该怎么做，靠你了…

**Cyrene:** Not a problem! (Trailblazer) can just relax. By the way, speaking of "joining hands"...

> **昔涟：**没问题！开拓者只要放轻松就好啦。对了，说到「携手」……

- ▸ Don't let your guard down. Be prepared for the worst.

> ▸ 别大意，做好最坏的打算。

**Cyrene:** (Trailblazer) is always so serious, and that's why you always feel so reassuring. By the way, speaking of "joining hands"...

> **昔涟：**开拓者总是这么认真，所以才令人安心呢。对了，说到「携手」……

**Cyrene:** Can I please do something before we go on stage? ♪

> **昔涟：**在走上舞台前，请允许我再小小任性一次吧♪

**Cyrene:** Remember the joke we made back at the Grove, (Trailblazer)?

> **昔涟：**开拓者，还记得我们在树庭开过的玩笑吗？

**Cyrene:** Look.

> **昔涟：**你看……

**Cyrene:** Aren't my hands pretty now?

> **昔涟：**现在，人家的手是不是很漂亮呢？

**Cyrene:** What a fascinating feeling... Like countless memories all swelling up at once.

> **昔涟：**真是奇妙的感觉…一时间，仿佛有无数回忆一起涌上心头。

**Cyrene:** What about you, (Trailblazer)? What did you feel when we touched each other?

> **昔涟：**开拓者，你呢？当我们彼此触碰，你又感受到了什么？

- ▸ You feel very tangible. Very real.

> ▸ 很真实的触感。

**Cyrene:** Really? That makes me really happy! If memories can move people, then it must've been a happy memory, right?

> **昔涟：**是吗？那我会很开心！如果一缕记忆能带给人触动，那代表她一定是幸福的，对吧？

- ▸ I'm a bit nervous...

> ▸ 有点惊慌…

**Cyrene:** Are you nervous? Me too, actually. (Trailblazer) and I weren't so reserved around each other when I was still Mem.

> **昔涟：**感到紧张吗？其实我也是。明明还是「迷迷」的时候，我和开拓者都不会这么矜持。

**Cyrene:** Human interactions are truly fascinating.

> **昔涟：**人和人的相处，真是不可思议呀。

- ▸ It's just a hand. No biggie.

> ▸ 只是一只手。

**Cyrene:** Hahaha, (Trailblazer) is way too honest! But that innocence is what makes you so uniquely charming.

> **昔涟：**嘻，开拓者也太诚实了吧？不过，这种纯粹正是你独一无二的魅力。

**Cyrene:** I'm gonna make it sound more romantic. It means even a bond formed over one touch is enough to transcend space-time.

> **昔涟：**让人家换种更浪漫的说法吧：即便只是一次对掌的牵绊，也足以跨越时空，对吧？

**Cyrene:** Now, please remember what you're feeling right now, write it down in your "heart," and keep it there.

> **昔涟：**那，就请铭记下此刻的感受，把它写在名为「心」的书页中，保存起来吧。

**Cyrene:** Memories you want to treasure will never fade away. Even if immersed in the long river of time, these memories will still leave traces of their existence.

> **昔涟：**想要珍藏的记忆，一定不会轻易逝去。即使被时光的长河磨洗，也会留下存在的痕迹。

**Cyrene:** So, if the view in your memory ever grows fuzzy, just reach out and touch it.

> **昔涟：**所以，如果记忆中的风景模糊了，就伸手去触碰它。

**Cyrene:** It's like reading bedtime stories together at night. It wipes away the chill of passing time, sharpens the outline of memory, and restores its power to nourish the spirit.

> **昔涟：**就像每一个共读睡前故事的夜晚，抹去岁月的霜，擦亮回忆的轮廓，让它重新成为心灵的力量。

**Cyrene:** Now, I'll carry this power with me... and for a little while, I must part from you, as I step into the long cycles of this world.

> **昔涟：**接下来，人家就要带上这份力量，短暂离开你的身边，走进漫长的循环啦。

**Cyrene:** Give me your blessings, (Trailblazer). Then...

> **昔涟：**祝福我吧，开拓者。然后……

**Cyrene:** Let that bright future be "As We Have Written" ♪

> **昔涟：**让那崭新的未来，如「我们」所书♪

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Cyrene:** Then... I'll leave it to you?

> **昔涟：**那…交给你啦？

**Zandar:** You wish to use Time as a weapon...

> **「赞达尔」：**你想以「岁月」为武器……

**Zandar:** To fight me?

> **「赞达尔」：**来与我抗衡？

**Zandar:** That is futile.

> **「赞达尔」：**这是徒劳。

**Zandar:** My existence...

> **「赞达尔」：**我的存在……

**Zandar:** Supersedes Amphoreus's cycles.

> **「赞达尔」：**超乎翁法罗斯的轮回。

**Cyrene:** All plays are like this.

> **昔涟：**任何剧目都是如此……

**Cyrene:** Once you're on the stage...

> **昔涟：**一旦登上台前……

**Cyrene:** ...It's hard to return to the audience.

> **昔涟：**……就难以退回观众。

**Cyrene:** Welcome to "our" story.

> **昔涟：**欢迎来到「我们」的故事……

**Cyrene:** How does it feel?

> **昔涟：**感觉如何？

*(Cutscene ends)*

**"Zandar":** ...

> **「赞达尔」：**……

**"Zandar":** Even the authority of Time cannot command the entire Scepter, nor can it plunge the Amphorean extrapolation into a loop...

> **「赞达尔」：**即便「岁月」的权能，也无法调动整台权杖，令翁法罗斯的演算陷入循环……

**"Zandar":** If this was no system malfunction, then was I implanted with a memetic entity of the Remembrance?

> **「赞达尔」：**这并非系统紊乱，而是我被植入了「记忆」的模因么？

**Cyrene:** You guessed it right away! As expected from someone people call the First Genius.

> **昔涟：**一下子就看穿了！不愧是大家口中的「第一位天才」呢。

**Cyrene:** I'm gonna keep doing what I just did if you don't stop, you know? (Trailblazer) and I won't allow you to leave this "memory" no matter what happens...

> **昔涟：**如果你再肆意妄为，刚才那一幕还会不断上演。无论如何，我和开拓者都不会让你离开这段「记忆」……

**Cyrene:** Which means you are now trapped, Lord Zandar.

> **昔涟：**「赞达尔」阁下，你已经身陷囹圄了哦？

**"Zandar":** An elegant idea and an exquisite execution. Regrettably, it is you who are restrained by time, not me.

> **「赞达尔」：**优雅的构思，精妙的执行。可惜，被时间掣肘的并非我，而是你。

**"Zandar":** Know that patience is my greatest weapon. "Waiting" and "victory" are the same for me.

> **「赞达尔」：**你应当理解，耐心是鄙人最强有力的武器。于我而言，「等待」和「胜利」是相同的概念。

**"Zandar":** You will crumble before I do in this moment stretched out to an eternity, just like Khaslana will. Yet all I need to do is to wait, as yet another futile bug falls down the cliff and into the bottomless abyss.

> **「赞达尔」：**和卡厄斯兰那一样，在那近乎永恒的刹那中，最先崩溃的会是你。而我只需等待——等待另一只徒劳的若虫跌下悬崖，坠入深渊。

**Cyrene:** Now I'm a pink bug? My looks are seriously changing all the time...

> **昔涟：**这次又是「粉色小虫」吗？人家的形象也太多变啦……

**Cyrene:** But you're not wrong. We're definitely not gonna solve the problem if we just put you in another prison.

> **昔涟：**但你说的没错。如果只是打造另一座牢笼，问题的本质并不会被解决。

**Cyrene:** So that's why we never wanted to do that in the first place!

> **昔涟：**所以，我们原本也没打算这么做哦？

**"Zandar":** ...

> **「赞达尔」：**……

**Screwllum:** I shall answer your confusion purely out of my respect for those who came before me. Have you heard of the Synesthesia Dreamscape in the Asdana system? Interestingly, in the distant past, that place once had a prison, too.

> **螺丝咕姆：**仅作为对先行者的敬意，由我来解答您的困惑：阁下是否听说过阿斯德纳星系的「联觉梦境」？很有趣，在久远的过去，那里也有一座监狱。

**Screwllum:** Lifeforms living in that environment abundant with memoria have a chance to connect their perceptions with each other's. Even across great spatial divides, they're able to detect shifts in one another's states. I call this phenomenon "Memory Zone Entanglement."

> **螺丝咕姆：**在忆质充盈的环境下，生命体知觉有一定概率产生某种关联。即便在空间上相隔极远，也能感知彼此的状态变化。我将这种现象称为「忆域纠缠」。

**Screwllum:** In Asdana, people made use of that phenomenon to build a nation within a dream... after adding in the catalysts of the Harmony and Remembrance, that is.

> **螺丝咕姆：**在阿斯德纳，人们利用这种现象——加以「同谐」和「记忆」的触媒——建造了一片梦中的国度。

**"Zandar":** Is that how you walked into my prison cell? Using those same principles?

> **「赞达尔」：**恰如此时此刻，你们运用相同的原理，步入了我所在的牢笼？

**Screwllum:** I would much rather call this location "our negotiation table," Lord Zandar. Nonetheless, your hypothesis is correct. We happen to have an ally skilled in "tuning," while (Trailblazer) and Miss Cyrene...

> **螺丝咕姆：**我更愿意将其称作「谈判席」，赞达尔阁下。但您的理解没错。我们恰好有一位擅长「调律」的盟友，而开拓者和昔涟小姐……

**Screwllum:** ...both have an astonishing amount of resolve, which I'm sure you can testify to. I am also sure that with the support of such a resolute memosprite, our conversation can last for a long, long time.

> **螺丝咕姆：**如你所见，他们的决心令人叹服。有如此坚定的「忆灵」相助，我们的对话——也将持续很久。

**Cyrene:** I'm the best at "listening to stories," you know.

> **昔涟：**只是「听故事」而已，人家最擅长这个了。

**Cyrene:** I'll leave the exciting clash of the Erudition to you two geniuses.

> **昔涟：**那，激动人心的「智识」交锋，就拜托两位天才啦？

**Herta:** A brainstorm? I like it. And it's two against one? I like it even more.

> **黑塔：**头脑风暴，我喜欢。还是二打一，更喜欢了。

**Herta:** Let's talk, Zandar. Take a guess. How much do you reckon we can dig out of your brain before the next second? And how many ways can we find to deal with you?

> **黑塔：**来谈谈吧，赞达尔，猜猜在指针走过下一秒前，我们能在你的脑袋里挖出多少东西，又能想出多少种解决你的办法？

**Herta:** Besides, in your current predicament, there's no way you can take our trump card away.

> **黑塔：**况且，现在的你——根本没机会抽走我们的王牌哪。

### Say goodbye to Hysilens

> *The curtain call music has ended. You must use the Coreflame to burn down this empty theater, then raise the curtain for the next act. Will it be an absurd tragedy, or a preposterous comedy? Only by moving forward can we glimpse what lies ahead, while everything behind us faces another final bow. But there's no need for regret or lingering attachment, for generations fall like leaves, and so, too, do heroes.*

**Herta:** Go, little one. Go overturn the "Era Nova"!

> **黑塔：**去吧，小家伙，扭转「再创世」——！

**Hysilens:** Lycurgus... disappeared?

> **海瑟音：**吕枯耳戈斯…消失了？

**Hysilens:** ...Heh, it seems you had conducted a secret hunt in the depths that even I cannot reach.

> **海瑟音：**…呵，看来在我无从企及的深水里，你们进行了一场隐秘的狩猎啊。

**Hysilens:** In that case... go, swim onward and give this campaign a fitting end.

> **海瑟音：**既然如此，向前游吧，去给这段征途一个恰如其分的结局。

**Hysilens:** I shall send you off with my song, gray fry. May this song penetrate time... and become the prelude to the new world.

> **海瑟音：**让我用歌声为你饯行，灰鱼儿。愿这歌声能穿透岁月…成为新世界的序曲。

*(Unlock Achievement God of Vengeance)*

### Return the Coreflame of Worldbearing

*(Talk to Hysilens, optional)*

She pours her heart and soul into singing you farewell.

For a thousand years, this melody has never ceased in Styxia.

*(Submit the Coreflame)*

**Cerydra:** Dux Gladiorum, you have completed your duty.

> **刻律德菈：**剑旗爵，你的任务已经完成了。

**Cerydra:** A new dawn is about to rise. Why not swim towards it?

> **刻律德菈：**新的黎明即将升起，何不向着它的光芒游去？

**Hysilens:** ...

> **海瑟音：**……

**Hysilens:** You died long ago, Imperator. Depart from my mind.

> **海瑟音：**你早已死去了，凯撒。离开我的心吧。

**Cerydra:** But your duty towards the Imperator did not die with me.

> **刻律德菈：**但你对凯撒的忠诚没有随我一同死去。

**Cerydra:** That duty will bind you no more. You will be able to hold for yourself one grandiose feast after another in the endless days to come.

> **刻律德菈：**现在，它不会再拘束你了。今后的无数个日夜，你尽可为自己操办一场又一场盛大的宴会……

**Cerydra:** So go. Go and swim towards that ocean beyond the sky with the Deliverer.

> **刻律德菈：**去吧，去和那救世主一同游往天外的大海。

**Hysilens:** Is this...

> **海瑟音：**这是……

**Cerydra:** A page out of that sidekick's book. I commanded her to gift this page to you when everything finally reaches the end.

> **刻律德菈：**是那小跟班书中的一页。我对她下令，当一切行至终点时，要将这页留赠予你。

**Hysilens:** What's on this page?

> **海瑟音：**这书页里有什么？

**Cerydra:** Memories. Lifelike memories.

> **刻律德菈：**记忆，栩栩如生的记忆。

**Cerydra:** That sidekick found me after the bloody feast a thousand years ago and showed me her diary.

> **刻律德菈：**千年前，那场血腥的宴会后，那小跟班找到了我，为我展示了她的日记本。

**Cerydra:** I saw within that notebook...

> **刻律德菈：**在那书中，我看见……

*(Enter Exploratory Excursion Epistle of Remembrance)*

### Review the Epistle of Remembrance in Imperator's name

> *Complete your conquest in the ocean at the boundaries of this world, and eternally slumber in the sound of tides. Eternally slumber in the sound of tides, and complete your conquest in the ocean at the boundaries of this world. They were never terrified of this prophecy. Rather, they somewhat looked forward to it. Back then, they stood on the shores of Loukas, watching the yet-unextinguished sun sink into the sea, imagining the day when the prophecy would come true. But she didn't know that the sea she dreamed of wasn't just a small pond. Nor was it just an empty shore. She didn't know that she had no desire for eternal rest. And what she truly yearned for was never a conquest. Thinking of the scenes that were so out of place, they watched together as the sun slowly, ever so slowly, sank into the sea, as if it would never rise again.*

Light Calendar 3960, before the campaign against Phagousa

**Cerydra:** Is this... that Deliverer's experience? It's truly immersive.

> **刻律德菈：**这是…那救世主的经历？真是身临其境。

**Cyrene:** People beyond the sky apparently utilize this power to seal past scenery into crystals they call "Light Cones."

> **昔涟：**据说，天外的人们会借助这种力量，将过去的风景存入名为「光锥」的水晶。

**Cyrene:** But I only know so much about the Remembrance, so I can't completely replicate the past... But I'll do my best to talk about the missing parts.

> **昔涟：**不过，人家对「记忆」的理解也浅尝辄止，没办法完整地复现过去。但我会尽力用讲述代替那些缺失的部分。

**Cerydra:** Heh. I'm all ears. Go on then. Do your best. But if your chatter gets too boring or if I deem it offensive, then Law will become forever beyond your reach.

> **刻律德菈：**呵，我洗耳恭听。全力以赴吧：倘若讲得太过无趣，又或失言，你们就要和「律法」失之交臂了。

**Cyrene:** Got it! I'm sure you'll find this to your liking. After all... It's human nature to want to embark on a journey into the unknown.

> **昔涟：**嗯！我相信您一定会满意的，毕竟…迈向未知的前路，从来都是人的本能呀。

*(Browse memories)*

**Cerydra:** What brightly colored annals.

> **刻律德菈：**还真是本色泽明艳的史书。

**Cerydra:** The journey of the Deliverer's conquest, heh... Let me partake in it.

> **刻律德菈：**「救世主」的征途…让我拜读一番吧。

**Cerydra:** Is this... A fortress in the sky?

> **刻律德菈：**这是…一座天空中的堡垒？

**Cyrene:** This is the first step of (Trailblazer)'s journey among the starry seas. This place is called the Herta Space Station. Ummm... Just think of it as a supersized Grove of Epiphany.

> **昔涟：**这是开拓者遨游星海的第一站，叫做「黑塔」空间站。嗯，就当它是一座超级大的神悟树庭吧。

**Cerydra:** Oh. So it's the territory of that witch. She and another Antikytheran lent me a helping hand back at the banquet.

> **刻律德菈：**喔，原来是那女巫的地盘。宴会上，是她和另一位安提基色拉人助我解了围。

**Cyrene:** Look, over there. Looks like (Trailblazer) is talking to Madam Herta in this memory.

> **昔涟：**那边——这段记忆里，开拓者好像在和黑塔女士聊天呢。

**Cyrene:** Let's go listen to what she thinks about this world, all constructed by Aeons and Paths...

> **昔涟：**听听看吧？她是如何看待这个由星神和命途构建的世界……

### Contemplate the proverb of the witch from beyond the sky

> *Complete your conquest in the ocean at the boundaries of this world, and eternally slumber in the sound of tides. Eternally slumber in the sound of tides, and complete your conquest in the ocean at the boundaries of this world. They were never terrified of this prophecy. Rather, they somewhat looked forward to it. Back then, they stood on the shores of Loukas, watching the yet-unextinguished sun sink into the sea, imagining the day when the prophecy would come true. But she didn't know that the sea she dreamed of wasn't just a small pond. Nor was it just an empty shore. She didn't know that she had no desire for eternal rest. And what she truly yearned for was never a conquest. Thinking of the scenes that were so out of place, they watched together as the sun slowly, ever so slowly, sank into the sea, as if it would never rise again.*

**Cerydra:** Are you telling me THAT woman is Herta?

> **刻律德菈：**你说那女人是黑塔？

**Cerydra:** She seems... shorter than I remember.

> **刻律德菈：**似乎…比印象里矮小些哪。

*(Approach the Trailblazer and Herta)*

**Herta:** Pretty nice space station, huh, (Trailblazer)? But just wait. After a couple more stops on the Astral Express, you'll see sights that'll make this place look ordinary.

> **黑塔：**这座空间站很漂亮吧，开拓者？不过，等你跟着星穹列车再走远点儿——不出两站吧——就会知道这景色也不算什么。

**Herta:** The cosmos is so vast that the "unknowns" far outnumber the "knowns," but none of them will end up being anything amazing. The farther you go, the more you'll understand what I mean.

> **黑塔：**银河很大，大到「未知」远远多于「已知」，但它们最后都算不上什么。你走得越远，越能理解这句话。

**Herta:** So do your best with the Nameless. Break a leg. And remember to send an update or two to Asta when you've got some downtime.

> **黑塔：**所以，跟着无名客好好干吧。没事记得给艾丝妲发个消息。

**Cerydra:** She certainly shares my way of thinking...

> **刻律德菈：**此人想法倒是与我一致……

**Cerydra:** Only that, instead of a conquest through blood and fire, they chose a gentler path.

> **刻律德菈：**只不过，比起血与火的征服，他们选择了更温和的道路。

**Cyrene:** People call Madam Herta and Mister Screwllum "geniuses." Apparently, only the smartest people in the cosmos can receive the blessings of Nous, Amphoreus's true creator.

> **昔涟：**黑塔女士和螺丝咕姆先生，被人们称为「天才」。据说只有银河中最具智慧的人，才能得到翁法罗斯真正的造物主「博识尊」的青睐。

**Cerydra:** Do you mean that even individuals of such nobility are merely subordinates to a god?

> **刻律德菈：**身份如此高贵，也只是神明的附庸么？

**Cyrene:** On the contrary, after all, it was the very first of them who created the god. But I don't really know all the details about that...

> **昔涟：**恰恰相反：是他们中的第一位创造了神明呢。但具体细节人家就不清楚了……

**Cyrene:** You might be able to ask Madam Herta herself when you get the chance.

> **昔涟：**有机会的话，说不定您可以问问黑塔女士本人。

**Cyrene:** Let's go check out some other worlds next, shall we?

> **昔涟：**接下来，让我们再去其他世界看看吧？

### Continue reviewing the Epistle of Remembrance

*(Listen to the two again, optional)*

**Herta:** The cosmos is so vast that the "unknowns" far outnumber the "knowns," but none of them will end up being anything amazing. The farther you go, the more you'll understand what I mean.

*(Browse memories)*

**Cyrene:** This next stop on the journey...

> **昔涟：**旅途的下一站……

**Cyrene:** Has a lot of similarities to Amphoreus.

> **昔涟：**和翁法罗斯有不少相似之处呢。

**Cerydra:** A snow-covered land? Interesting.

> **刻律德菈：**一座雪国？有趣。

**Cyrene:** This star is named Jarilo-VI. Aww, that name sounds so cold... Belobog sounds better.

> **昔涟：**这颗星星叫做「雅利洛-VI」。啊，这个名字冷冰冰的，还是「贝洛伯格」更好听。

**Cyrene:** The girl we see here holds a similar station to you, Imperator. She's the Supreme Guardian of this world, a leader who took up the mantle of government during a disaster.

> **昔涟：**眼前的女孩，身份和凯撒相似——她是这个世界的「大守护者」，是一位在灾难中挺身而出的领袖。

**Cerydra:** I did not expect the empress of this world to be so young.

> **刻律德菈：**看不出来，这个世界的女皇竟如此年幼。

**Cyrene:** Well, your appearance isn't much older...

> **昔涟：**只谈论外表，凯撒也不遑多让呢……

### Contemplate the confessions of the Snow Country's Sovereign

> *Complete your conquest in the ocean at the boundaries of this world, and eternally slumber in the sound of tides. Eternally slumber in the sound of tides, and complete your conquest in the ocean at the boundaries of this world. They were never terrified of this prophecy. Rather, they somewhat looked forward to it. Back then, they stood on the shores of Loukas, watching the yet-unextinguished sun sink into the sea, imagining the day when the prophecy would come true. But she didn't know that the sea she dreamed of wasn't just a small pond. Nor was it just an empty shore. She didn't know that she had no desire for eternal rest. And what she truly yearned for was never a conquest. Thinking of the scenes that were so out of place, they watched together as the sun slowly, ever so slowly, sank into the sea, as if it would never rise again.*

**Cerydra:** The object in the distance resembles Nikador. Is it a weapon?

> **刻律德菈：**远处那貌似尼卡多利的，是兵器？

**Cyrene:** No. It's called... Uhm... Right! The Geological Reconstruction Unit!

> **昔涟：**不是哦。它的名字是…是…对了，地质改造工程单元！

**Cerydra:** Just Georios's Titankin? Disappointing.

> **刻律德菈：**只是吉奥里亚的眷属啊，令人失望。

*(Approach the (Trailblazer) and Bronya)*

**Bronya:** It's all thanks to you that we survived the crisis. The collaboration with the IPC is both an opportunity and a challenge... Nonetheless, it's an important step in our journey back into the universe.

> **布洛妮娅：**多亏你们的帮助，我们才能顺利度过危机。与公司的合作，既是机遇，也是挑战…但无论如何，都是我们回归宇宙的重要一步。

**Cyrene:** This world was sealed beneath its sky just like Amphoreus was, and they only managed to reconnect with the stars when the Nameless arrived...

> **昔涟：**这个世界和翁法罗斯很像，也被关在天幕下，直到无名客到来，才得以回归群星……

**Cerydra:** That "IPC" she spoke of... Is it a more powerful organization?

> **刻律德菈：**她口中的「公司」…听上去是个更大的势力？

**Cyrene:** Yes. The IPC conquered many worlds through trade and opened up a vast territory that way. There are many such powerful alliances beyond Amphoreus, and they all walk on roads different from the Destruction.

> **昔涟：**是的。他们用财富的流通征服了许多世界，开辟了辽阔的疆域。在翁法罗斯之外，有许多这般强大的同盟，它们行于和「毁灭」截然不同的道路。

**Cyrene:** Coming back to the Supreme Guardian and her Belobog... Didn't they find a peaceful way to enter the interstellar community?

> **昔涟：**回到大守护者和她的贝洛伯格——您看，他们不也找到了和平走向群星的方法么？

**Cerydra:** That cannot be true. That "IPC" would never have grown so big on trade alone. It may pretend to be a wealthy merchant with clean hands, but I am willing to bet that it grew fat on gory oppression and bloodshed.

> **刻律德菈：**这是谬论。那「公司」不可能单凭财货实现如此伟业。就算它表面上十足体面，内里也必定沾染着血腥的杀戮和剥削。

**Cerydra:** Its path of conquest must have had no mercy for the weak and would have destroyed them if needed. Are you telling me that this snowland never thought about tossing its dignity to the wind and kneeling to the IPC? I will not believe it.

> **刻律德菈：**而在它的征服之路上，弱者必遭欺凌，甚至毁灭——难道这冰球在面对公司时就没有想过舍弃尊严，卑躬屈膝？我不相信。

**Cerydra:** I will never allow the Imperator's empire to be sullied by others. Amphoreus must become a power so strong that no interstellar foreigner would dare to look down upon us.

> **刻律德菈：**我绝不允许凯撒的帝国任人宰割。翁法罗斯必须成为强权，不容寰宇轻视。

**Cyrene:** Imperator... Belobog isn't as weak as you imagine. Listen.

> **昔涟：**但是，凯撒，贝洛伯格没有您想的那样软弱…不信，您听？

**Bronya:** ...I indeed hesitated, but now I understand what we must do. Belobog must learn how to walk on her own, until she no longer needs the support of others, and can stand on her two feet among the stars.

> **布洛妮娅：**…我确实产生过动摇，但现在已经明白：贝洛伯格必须学会行走、奔跑，最终脱离他人的搀扶，自立于星间。

**Bronya:** Even if I may not live to see it, I believe such a day will surely come.

> **布洛妮娅：**我相信那天一定会到来，哪怕我无法亲眼目睹。

**Cyrene:** They still have their dignity intact. One day, that hope will melt through the ice and help them into a prosperous tomorrow.

> **昔涟：**你看，他们心中仍有尊严。有朝一日，这份希望能融化坚冰，助他们走向繁荣。

**Cerydra:** But the order of the cosmos is already set in stone. The old laws decree that only the strong survive, binding the weak in an unbroken chain, until they wither and turn to dust. Unless... they embrace the power of the Destruction.

> **刻律德菈：**可惜，银河的秩序早已铸成。若不倚仗「毁灭」，弱者只能自限于弱肉强食的旧律，直到化为尘埃。

**Cerydra:** We... No... Amphoreus must shatter the old law and become the new order among the stars.

> **刻律德菈：**翁法罗斯，我们…必须击碎旧律，成为星间新的秩序。

**Cyrene:** Speaking of the Order... Another one of (Trailblazer)'s companions may be able to enlighten you further on that.

> **昔涟：**啊，既然说到了「秩序」…开拓者的一位旅伴，或许能为您带来启发。

**Cyrene:** Let's go to the next page.

> **昔涟：**一起翻开下一页吧？

> *Complete your conquest in the ocean at the boundaries of this world, and eternally slumber in the sound of tides. Rather, they somewhat looked forward to it. Back then, they stood on the shores of Loukas, watching the yet-unextinguished sun sink into the sea, imagining the day when the prophecy would come true. But she didn't know that the sea she dreamed of wasn't just a small pond. Nor was it just an empty shore. She didn't know that she had no desire for eternal rest. And what she truly yearned for was never a conquest. *(Approach the Nameless and Sunday)*

**Sunday:** I wish to gaze upon the views of the Reverie Hotel one more time, (Trailblazer).

> **星期日：**我想再多看一眼这「白日梦」酒店的光景，开拓者。

**Sunday:** I'll be just a traveler when I come back here again. And perhaps, through a traveler's eyes, even the things I once knew so well will feel strangely different.

> **星期日：**下一次回到这里，我便是一名纯粹的过客，透过旅行者的眼眸，我无比熟悉的一切或许也会变得十分不同吧？

**Cyrene:** Penacony is a land of the Harmony where all dreams and hopes merge and combine. It believes in unrestrained freedom, and that's how it lost itself in shallow delights...

> **昔涟：**匹诺康尼是汇聚一切理想的「同谐」之地。它主张不容干涉的自由，因而在声色犬马中步入迷茫……

**Cyrene:** Then, this young man before you chose to rewrite its laws using the power of the Order, hoping to shape a dream-bound paradise of perfect happiness.

> **昔涟：**于是，您眼前这位少年选择用「秩序」的力量谱写律法，希望能在梦中塑造一处绝对幸福的圣地。

**Sunday:** Yet the paradise of my dreams remains far away. Perhaps a brief journey with the Nameless can show me more possibilities in my search for paradise.

> **星期日：**但我理想中的乐园依旧遥远。或许，暂且与各位无名客同行，能令我的求索之路展现更多可能。

**Sunday:** As for what Penacony should look like, it'll be up to her... no, up to their "trailblazing" now.

> **星期日：**至于匹诺康尼的未来应是何种样貌，就由她…他们来「开拓」吧。

**Cerydra:** It sounds like this ideal kingdom is no more.

> **刻律德菈：**听起来，这座理想之邦已经成为了过去式。

**Cyrene:** That's right. The people chose to wake up at the end. You can't see tomorrow when drowning in sweet dreams.

> **昔涟：**嗯，因为沉沦于美梦是看不到明天的，人们最终选择了从梦中醒来。

**Cerydra:** This individual's "Law" acknowledged only gods and constructs, leaving no space for humanity. And yet, the fact that he could accept defeat and resume his journey shows a rare self-awareness and clarity of mind.

> **刻律德菈：**此人的「律法」中只有神和造物，但未留给「人」一席之地。能接受一时失败，再度踏上旅程，证明他活得很清醒。

**Cyrene:** You are also very self-aware, Imperator.

> **昔涟：**您看得也很清楚呀。

**Cyrene:** Then you must know that the world promised by the Destruction also has no place for "humanity."

> **昔涟：**那您应该知道，在「毁灭」许诺的世界里，同样没有给「人」留下余地。

**Cerydra:** ...

> **刻律德菈：**……

**Cerydra:** I am indeed very aware of Lycurgus's honeyed words of deceit. I have seen enough. Take me back.

> **刻律德菈：**吕枯耳戈斯口蜜腹剑，我再清楚不过。已经看得够多了，带我回去吧。

**Cyrene:** Then let's wrap up this trip down memory lane with one last scene.

**Cerydra:** This... absolute wonder...

> **刻律德菈：**这窗外的奇观，它是……

**Cyrene:** Isn't it pretty? That's our Amphoreus. Our world flows and meanders in this endless band of light.

**Cyrene:** Though we were born from the designs of the gods and are not yet truly alive... the Chrysos Heirs of Amphoreus still chose to stand against fate and carved a legend no different from that of humankind.

> **昔涟：**我们虽然在神明的设计中挣扎，尚且不是真正的生命…但，翁法罗斯的黄金裔依旧选择同命运抗争，书写了和「人」无异的神话。

**Cyrene:** Will you really renounce the heroes in the world's previous thirty million cycles, Imperator? Including even yourself? ...Will you bow to brutal destiny and allow that puppet master named the Destruction to have THEIR wish?

> **昔涟：**凯撒，您真的要否定过去三千万世的英雄们——连同您自己在内——屈服于暴虐的命运，让操弄一切的「毁灭」如愿以偿吗？

**Cerydra:** ...

> **刻律德菈：**……

**Cerydra:** I know what you want to do, sidekick. You want to use that Deliverer's memories to influence me and tilt the scales of law toward him/her and the two geniuses.

> **刻律德菈：**我知道你在想什么，小跟班。你想借那救世主的回忆打动我，令律法的天平倾向他/她和那两位天才，对么？

**Cyrene:** You really saw right through me, Imperator.

> **昔涟：**人家的心思，真是一下子就被凯撒看穿了呀。

**Cerydra:** How can one rule an empire if one is incapable of understanding people's minds?

> **刻律德菈：**若连人心都无法读懂，又何谈掌控帝国？

**Cerydra:** I have decided on Law's final destination. However, before I move my next chess piece...

> **刻律德菈：**「律法」的去向，我意已决。但在挪动下一步棋子前……

**Cerydra:** Before we go on, tell me this, sidekick. Throughout his/her "trailblaze"...

> **刻律德菈：**我还想在这记忆里确认一事。告诉我，小跟班，在他/她「开拓」的旅途中——

**Cerydra:** Was there ever a memory of the ocean?

> **刻律德菈：**可曾见过大海的面貌？

...

**Hysilens:** The ocean... My cradle. My home.

> **海瑟音：**大海啊。我的摇篮，我的故乡。

**Hysilens:** "You shall complete your conquest in the ocean at the boundaries of this world, and eternally slumber in the sound of tides..."

> **海瑟音：**「汝将于天地境界之海完成征服，长眠于涛声中——」

**Hysilens:** "You shall eternally slumber in the sound of tides, and complete your conquest in the ocean at the boundaries of this world..."

> **海瑟音：**「汝将长眠于涛声中，于天地境界之海完成征服——」

**Hysilens:** Our prophecies aligned as flawlessly as a sea snake circling upon itself... an elegant loop, like a joyful hymn.

> **海瑟音：**预言如海蛇首尾相衔，多么优美，好似欢歌……

**Hysilens:** Like the circulating ocean current. Like our two fates. Like Amphoreus itself.

> **海瑟音：**正如翁法罗斯，一同你我二人的命运，像那回环的海流。

### Witness the Imperator's great journey from start to finish

Light Calendar 3960, at the end of the campaign against Phagousa

**Hysilens:** The trial is over. So, tell me...

> **海瑟音：**试炼已经结束了。现在，告诉我吧……

**Hysilens:** Who will own the divine authority of "Law"?

> **海瑟音：**「律法」的神权，将会落入何人之手？

**Cerydra:** I won't give it to anyone.

> **刻律德菈：**我不会将它交给任何人。

**Hysilens:** What?

> **海瑟音：**什么？

**Cerydra:** I am the "Law." Why should I give my imperial crown to any other?

> **刻律德菈：**我即是「律法」。凯撒的冠冕，岂有拱手相让的道理？

**Cerydra:** Both the Deliverer and the Theoros came from beyond the sky. They have no right to interfere with the lands I conquered with my own two hands. The "Law" of Amphoreus shall be determined by Amphoreus's master.

> **刻律德菈：**救世主也好，神礼官也罢，皆从天外而来，无权干涉我亲自征服的土地。翁法罗斯的「律法」，自当由她的主人裁定。

**Hysilens:** ...

> **海瑟音：**……

**Hysilens:** What exactly did you see in the trial?

> **海瑟音：**你究竟在试炼中看到了什么？

**Cerydra:** Everything. I saw everything regarding the rules of this world's operation. I saw the laws of extrapolation that Theoros spoke of... I saw the "Ultimate Protocol."

> **刻律德菈：**一切。关于这世界运行规则的一切，那神礼官口中的演算法则…「终极协议」。

**Cerydra:** As long as I offer a sacrifice of equal weight, I can rewrite the laws of this world according to my will. I can turn good into evil, ugliness into beauty, and weakness into strength...

> **刻律德菈：**只需献上等价的供物，我便能以自身意志改写此世的法则。善可以为恶，丑可以为美，弱可以为强……

**Cerydra:** Fate gave me a very fair deal.

> **刻律德菈：**而命运开出的价码非常公平。

**Cerydra:** One rule change... for the life of one demigod.

> **刻律德菈：**改写一条律令，仅需一位半神的性命。

**Hysilens:** What... are you planning to do?

> **海瑟音：**你打算…做什么？

**Cerydra:** You can't guess? We can conquer the stars without relying on any power from beyond the sky. We just need to make some reasonable sacrifices.

> **刻律德菈：**答案显而易见。无须仰仗天外伟力，我们也可以征服群星——只需付出一些合理的牺牲。

**Hysilens:** Your ambitions have already made so many people give up their lives for the "Law"! And you're still not done!?

> **海瑟音：**已经有那么多人为你的野心，为「律法」付出了生命的代价，你却仍不满足？

**Cerydra:** I am determined for Amphoreus to stand on her own two feet among the stars.

> **刻律德菈：**我意已决，翁法罗斯必须自立于群星。

**Hysilens:** You are beyond reason... You think such a simple and hypocritical statement can justify your atrocities!?

> **海瑟音：**不可理喻。你以为一句轻飘飘的伪善之词，就能给自己的暴行开脱…？！

**Cerydra:** "There's no new without destroying the old." That's what you consider an atrocity? Ironic, for that is also the unchanging law of Amphoreus's revolutions.

> **刻律德菈：**「不破不立」——这就是你口中所谓的暴行。讽刺的是，它同样也是翁法罗斯变革的铁律。

**Cerydra:** Ever since the Flame-Chase prophecy resounded in the distance, fate has decreed for the Chrysos Heirs to ignite our blood and illuminate this dark age. People would still be puppets of the old dynasty if it weren't for my conquests.

> **刻律德菈：**自那逐火的神谕在远方响起，命运就注定了黄金裔要燃烧鲜血，照亮这个黑暗的时代。可若没有我的征服，人们就只是旧王朝的提线木偶。

**Hysilens:** But your rule doesn't seem to be any different from your predecessors'. Bloodshed, tyranny, oppression...

> **海瑟音：**但现在看来，你的征途也不出前人：血腥、暴虐，满是压迫……

**Hysilens:** You're making the same mistakes they did.

> **海瑟音：**你不过是在重蹈覆辙。

**Cerydra:** Go and see everything for yourself! Go to any library I've built. Go listen to the rumors on every street corner. Go and witness how the Imperator crushed the old laws and forged new ones. Go and see what my conquests brought to this world.

> **刻律德菈：**那就亲自看吧！去我建造的图书馆也好，到街头巷尾寻找流言也罢——去看看凯撒踏碎旧律、铸造新律的一生，看我的征服为这个世界带来了什么。

**Cerydra:** I cleansed the Chrysos War's filth and mended all the conflicts left by the Era Bellica. I restored Okhema's might and allowed mortals and Chrysos Heirs to face each other as equals in the Council. I punished all sins and rewarded all achievements...

> **刻律德菈：**我涤荡了黄金战争的污垢，弥合了纷争世以来的所有分歧；我恢复了奥赫玛的权威，让凡人和黄金裔平等站上议院的讲坛；我惩罚了一切罪恶，赞赏了一切功绩……

**Cerydra:** I smashed Talanton's shackles, united humankind to save themselves, and started the Flame-Chase Era. That's what the Imperator has done in her glorious life.

> **刻律德菈：**是我砸碎了塔兰顿的枷锁，团结世人自救，开启了逐火的时代。这短短几句话，就是凯撒光辉的一生。

**Cerydra:** So, now, harken to my final words: History will eventually forget Cerydra, but it will never forget the Imperator's people or the Flame-Chase Era she began!

> **刻律德菈：**所以现在，听好我的遗言：历史总有一天会遗忘刻律德菈，但绝不会忘记凯撒的人民——和由她开启的逐火时代！

**Hysilens:** "Final words"...?

> **海瑟音：**「遗言」…？

**Cerydra:** ...

> **刻律德菈：**……

**Cerydra:** Amphoreus is about to be destroyed. There's no time left for pointless debates.

> **刻律德菈：**翁法罗斯濒临毁灭，已容不下无意义的争辩。

**Cerydra:** There is only one law I want to overturn to aid the final battle of deliverance, so I will only offer the life of one demigod...

> **刻律德菈：**为了这场救世之战，我要倾覆的「律法」只有一条，要献上的半神也只有一位——

**Cerydra:** In the name of the Imperator, I command you one last time, Helektra.

> **刻律德菈：**海列屈拉，我以凯撒之名，最后一次对你下令。

**Cerydra:** Either sacrifice yourself for conquest, or pierce this tyrant's chest with your sword.

> **刻律德菈：**为征服献身，或用剑刺入暴君的胸膛。

"Thus, the Imperator concluded her final speech."

"At the end of her life, at the last moment before she stepped onto the altar, she gave her most loyal subject the choice to decide fate, just as she promised her compatriots before her very first campaign."

**Cerydra:** "My blood flows in you now! Heroic compatriots, follow me, and we'll seize fate's reins!"

> **刻律德菈：**「我已将金血分给你们。现在，英勇的同胞，跟随我，成为命运的主人！」

"The Imperator fell. Some say that the Dux Gladiorum turned traitor and killed her liege. Some say that she imprisoned the tyrant in a shallow pond of water, then left... which meant the Imperator had to kill herself to rewrite the 'Law'..."

"There are countless stories about her death. All we can be sure of is, when the knight left the Vortex, the Imperator was still mumbling about their past conquests together..."

"...The beginning and the end of her existence."

**"Dux Fragoris" Labienus:** Imperator! Wait! How could someone of your status come to the front lines? Let me be the flag-bearer.

> **「断锋爵」拉比努斯：**不，凯撒！您尊贵无匹，怎能亲临前线？还是让我来担任旗手吧——

**Cerydra:** The troops defending the pass to the west of the Grove. Have they surrendered?

> **刻律德菈：**我问你，树庭西方关隘守军投降了吗？

**"Dux Fragoris" Labienus:** They ran as quickly as they could when they heard your name! The Golden Cocoon Pass is now in the First Legion's hands!

> **「断锋爵」拉比努斯：**他们一听到凯撒的威名就望风而逃，金茧关现在已经移交给第一军团了！

**Cerydra:** Good. What about the Dolosian militia?

> **刻律德菈：**很好，多洛斯人的游击队呢？

**"Dux Fragoris" Labienus:** The Third Legion set fire to the mountains and flattened everything west of the basin. They just need a few more days...

> **「断锋爵」拉比努斯：**第三军团两天前点燃了山火，盆地西方已经夷为平地，只要再过几天……

**Cerydra:** That is enough. We cannot afford to miss an opportunity for victory. Lastly, how many Ladonian cannons are left?

> **刻律德菈：**足够了，战机转瞬即逝，我们也等不起。最后，拉冬人的炮垒，现在还剩几座？

**"Dux Fragoris" Labienus:** They are no more. The Second and Fourth Legions have destroyed them all.

> **「断锋爵」拉比努斯：**不存在了——第二军团和第四军团已经把它们彻底荡平了！

**Cerydra:** Very good. They now have no way out, no reinforcements, and no artillery support. We will be able to liberate the Grove of Epiphany in the blink of an eye.

> **刻律德菈：**非常好。现在他们一无退路、二无援兵、三无火力支援，我军必能迅速解放神悟树庭。

**Cerydra:** We will launch the final charge one quint after the Curtain-Fall Hour tomorrow. Remember, as long as the alliance's flag still flies on my shoulders, you must keep moving forward.

> **刻律德菈：**总冲锋就在明日幕匿时过后一刻，记住：只要我肩头的同盟旗帜没有倒下，你们就绝不能放弃前进。

**Cerydra:** Take no prisoners among the enemy soldiers. All sages who resist are to be captured and tried according to martial law. And do not destroy any books. Now go! Notify the entire army!

> **刻律德菈：**敌军战士就地处决，反抗的哲人一律押解回营，依军法处置；还有，切不可损毁藏书——去吧，通告全军！

**"Dux Fragoris" Labienus:** Understood! Those who are resolved to die, we salute you!

> **「断锋爵」拉比努斯：**是！赴死者向您致敬！

**"Dux Brumalis" Seneca:** Reporting in! The Mountain Dwellers are resisting fiercely, and the legendary "Mountainbreaker" has personally engaged in battle. Their formations are nigh impenetrable...

> **「冬霖爵」塞涅卡：**报！山之民反抗猛烈，传闻中的「开山者」亲临战场，他们的战阵滴水不漏……

**Cerydra:** Then cease our frontal assault. Send scouts to all legions and tell them to retreat into the Tretos plains before the Parting Hour.

> **刻律德菈：**那就不必再强攻了。派斥候传讯给各军团，责令他们在离愁时内全部撤退到特雷托斯平原以内。

**"Dux Brumalis" Seneca:** Wha— Are you planning to surrender?

> **「冬霖爵」塞涅卡：**什么…您这是打算投降？

**Cerydra:** It's a strategic change of plans. The seismic walking dragon is enough for that battlefield.

> **刻律德菈：**这是战略转移。正面战场有那掣地的伏龙，足够了。

**"Dux Brumalis" Seneca:** Walking dragon... You mean that stupid beast Terravox? What can they even do?

> **「冬霖爵」塞涅卡：**伏龙…荒笛，那头愚蠢的地兽？它能做什么？

**Cerydra:** Stupid? No. They are the wisest of all of Georios's creations. Only they can perceive the currents of time.

> **刻律德菈：**愚蠢？不，它是吉奥里亚所有造物里最具智慧的，只有它能看清时代的流向。

**Cerydra:** Let them deal with their earthly brethren! We will advance and take the lead in defeating what remains of our enemy.

> **刻律德菈：**就让它亲自料理大地的同胞吧！我们要急行军，率先战胜残敌……

**Cerydra:** I will deliver the most fiery retribution for the humiliation they dealt us, and crown myself with a god-slaying wreath!

> **刻律德菈：**必须对敌人的羞辱给以最炽烈的报复，为给自己戴上弑神的桂冠！

**"Dux Brumalis" Seneca:** ...Understood. I'll see to it. Those who are resolved to die, we salute you!

> **「冬霖爵」塞涅卡：**…明白了，我这就去安排。赴死者向您致敬！

**"Dux Helkolithist" Apollonius:** The Temple of the Three Fates sent a letter, Imperator.

> **「曳石爵」阿波罗尼：**凯撒，命运三相殿来信了。

**"Dux Carminum" Verginia:** They refused to surrender and said this letter is their last warning to us.

> **「吟风爵」维吉妮娅：**他们拒绝了招降。还说，此次答复将作为对我们的最后通牒。

**Cerydra:** Hah...

> **刻律德菈：**呵……

**Cerydra:** Tell the commanding officers not to launch any attacks unless they receive the Imperator's handwritten approval. Disobedience will be punished by death.

> **刻律德菈：**去吧。告诉指挥官们，若没收到凯撒亲笔手谕，绝不得擅自发起进攻，否则格杀勿论。

**"Dux Carminum" Verginia:** Wh—What are you doing?

> **「吟风爵」维吉妮娅：**这…这是何意？

**Cerydra:** The priests of the Three Fates aren't soldiers, but politicians. Do not mistakenly believe they don't have a plan. These dying pests will wring out every idea from their brains all for short-sighted gain. They are much shrewder than we are.

> **刻律德菈：**命运三相的祭司不是军人，但是政治家。不要以为他们是在盲目抵抗，这群垂死的狗鼠会为自己的短视穷思竭虑，以至于比他们的敌人精明得多。

**Cerydra:** I am inferior to them in that regard. They also have the prophecy to wield as a weapon, something I am not blessed with. Launching an unplanned attack will only disadvantage us, as all of Amphoreus will judge our actions...

> **刻律德菈：**我不如他们——况且，神谕也是他们的战争机器，我没有这种好运。贸然开战只会令我们陷于不利，一举一动都将受翁法罗斯所有人审判……

**Cerydra:** Which means I will be heading to the battlefield myself, just as I have always done. This time, however, I will go alone. I will conduct secret negotiations to ensure fate is on our side.

> **刻律德菈：**所以，像往常一样，我会亲自出马。但，这次我将孤军深入，用一场秘密谈判让命运站在我们这边。

**"Dux Helkolithist" Apollonius:** Understood! The legions will make their preparations. Those who are resolved to die, we salute you!

> **「曳石爵」阿波罗尼：**是，各军团也将同时做好准备。赴死者向您致敬！

**Dux Goldweaver Aglaea:** Why are we not allowed to march out with the army, Imperator?

> **「金织爵」阿格莱雅：**凯撒，为何我们不在出征的名录中？

**Cerydra:** This will be a long voyage with no return. Those of you who bear the prophecy cannot come with me.

> **刻律德菈：**这是一场有去无回的远航。背负神谕的你们，不得随行。

**Dux Goldweaver Aglaea:** Why are you personally leading this battle if it's doomed to fail? The Flame-Chase Journey cannot be without a leader...

> **「金织爵」阿格莱雅：**若此战必将落败，您又为何要亲自出征？逐火之旅不能无人引领……

**Cerydra:** That is correct. The empire will be without a head when the Imperator falls. But remember, as long as you seize the right opportunities, even a chaotic game will remain winnable.

> **刻律德菈：**是啊，凯撒陨落，帝国必将群龙无首。但，记住了：只要能抓住机会，混乱的盘面就不会成为危局。

**Cerydra:** My departure will create a long staircase for you. You will understand what I mean when news of my death reaches your ears.

> **刻律德菈：**我的离去，会筑成你们的长阶——待我陨落的消息传扬开来，你终会明白这个道理。

**Dux Fatorum Tribbie:** Cerydra... Is our journey about to be over?

> **「命运爵」缇宝：**刻律德菈…我们的旅途，是要结束了吗？

**Cerydra:** Destroy the old laws and forge new ones... My conquest has reached its limits within the borders of Amphoreus. Since the prophecy already decreed that the Imperator would never conquer the stars as she wished, I will bow out here and try no more.

> **刻律德菈：**踏碎旧律，铸成新律…在翁法罗斯的界限内，我的征服已经抵达了尽头。既然神谕已经宣判，凯撒必无法遂愿征服星海，那我便就此退场，不再作他想。

**Cerydra:** More Chrysos Heirs bearing divine authority will be born, and many more heroes will arise...

> **刻律德菈：**在那之后，未来还会有背负神权的黄金裔，还有更多英雄降世……

**Cerydra:** Will they demand peace or war? Will they resist tyranny, or become the next Imperator? I do not care. The children of the future will decide for themselves.

> **刻律德菈：**要和平，还是要战争？要反抗暴政，还是成为下一个凯撒？我不在乎，后人自会做主。

**Cerydra:** Do not mourn for me. Anyone can kill the Imperator, but the only one who can truly destroy her...

> **刻律德菈：**不必为我惋惜：能杀死凯撒的人无穷无尽，但真正能毁灭她的……

**Cerydra:** Is Cerydra.

> **刻律德菈：**只有刻律德菈。

### Witness the legacy left by the Imperator

"At the end, I faithfully recorded the most important battles in the Imperator's life. Her conquest was about to be completed at this point in the story."

"However, for some reason, a memory suddenly entered my mind just as I was about to put down my pen."

"It was a journey on a fantastical train. I had the honor of having a heart-to-heart conversation with the Imperator under the stars, and I asked her this question at the end of that discussion..."

**Cyrene:** How do you want history to remember you? As a hero who gave up her life for the Flame-Chase... or as a tyrant?

> **昔涟：**您希望后人如何记住自己？为逐火献身的英雄…还是一位暴君？

"She answered so quickly, as if she had made up her mind long ago."

**Cerydra:** I would rather be forgotten than be defined.

> **刻律德菈：**我宁愿被遗忘，也不愿被定义。

**Cerydra:** Because the "Law" can be neither eternal nor unchallenged...

> **刻律德菈：**因为，「律法」既不可能永恒，也不可能唯一……

**Cerydra:** And only "humanity" can pen laws into history's pages.

> **刻律德菈：**能为历史书写下规则的，从来都惟有「人」。

**Cerydra:** Now, I have shattered the greatest "Law" of Amphoreus...

> **刻律德菈：**现在，翁法罗斯至伟大的「律法」，已被我踏破……

**Cerydra:** The game... is nearing its end...

> **刻律德菈：**棋局…该收官了……

**Cerydra:** Dux Gladiorum... even though... you killed the Imperator... in the previous thirty million cycles...

> **刻律德菈：**剑旗爵…明明…过去三千多万世…都是你亲手杀死了凯撒……

**Cerydra:** This time... you finally... found your own "Law"...

> **刻律德菈：**但这次…你的心中…终于找到自己的「律法」了哪……

**Cerydra:** Then... Hysilens... Helektra...

> **刻律德菈：**既然如此…海瑟音啊…海列屈拉……

**Cerydra:** I will ask someone to deliver... the prophecy's "ocean at the boundaries of this world"... to you...

> **刻律德菈：**那神谕中的「天地境界之海」…我会托人送往你的手中……

**Cerydra:** Will you... choose to... swim into that ocean...? I... do not care...

> **刻律德菈：**无论…你是否选择游向那里…我都不会在乎……

**Cerydra:** Do not... care...

> **刻律德菈：**不会……

**Cerydra:** ...

> **刻律德菈：**……

"The Imperator muttered about her conquests, and news of her death spread in the city."

**Spirited Elder:** Freedom! Liberty! The tyrant is dead! Go, shout this news in the streets!

> **振奋的元老：**自由！解放！暴君死了！去，到街上宣告这番要闻！

**Delighted Elder:** My people! My fellow Elders! Do not panic. Stand your ground. That usurper has paid for her ambition with her life!

> **喜悦的元老：**各位民众，各位元老啊，大家不要惊慌失措，都站定吧——那僭主已为野心偿债了！

**Cerydra:** People once cheered for my ascension... and now they cheer for my fall... No matter. I am fond of applause...

> **刻律德菈：**人们曾为我登峰欢呼…如今为陨落欢呼…也好，我钟爱欢呼……

**Cerydra:** Ah... I see... the cosmos... the battlefield that I cannot reach...

> **刻律德菈：**啊…我看到了…银河…我无法踏足的疆场啊……

**Cerydra:** I will return... in the next cycle... The stars will hear my legion's war drums... hear... the great name of... the Imperator...

> **刻律德菈：**来世…我必将归来…让群星听到军团的战鼓…听到凯撒的威名……

**Cerydra:** Hear... Amphoreus's...

> **刻律德菈：**听到…翁法罗斯……

**Cerydra:** Cery... dra...

> **刻律德菈：**刻律…德菈……

"To this day, we do not know which rule the Imperator overwrote at the cost of her life."

"Perhaps the truth will only be revealed at the moment of checkmate, just like every chess game she ever played."

"We only know her death inflamed Okhema. On one side of the city, those who defended liberty toppled her statues to the ground. On the other side, her supporters put themselves between the masses and her monuments, declaring loudly that 'the sovereign is still among the people.'"

"It is said that golden blood congealed into the same letters everywhere they fought. It was the name borne by Okhema's first and final sovereign: the 'Imperator' Cerydra."

"But the one who bore that name did not see any of this. Her gaze was cast far away, and the sound of tides in her ears drowned out the people's cries."

"What was the Imperator thinking about in that prophesied 'ocean at the boundaries of this world'? In that shallow puddle of stagnant water? Was she reminiscing about her past conquests, or repenting for the millions killed for the Flame-Chase?"

"It was neither. The Imperator only saw how the surface of the water gleamed like that crude chessboard, dissected by light and shadow, in the afternoon when her teacher played against her for the first time."

**Eccentric Chessmaster:** Look at this chessboard. What do the pieces look like?

> **古怪的弈者：**你看这面棋盘，这些棋子，像什么？

**Cerydra:** Heh. They are strangely beautiful...

> **刻律德菈：**呵，还真是美得奇异……

**Cerydra:** They look like... the stars in the night sky ablaze.

> **刻律德菈：**简直…就像夜空中的星星在熊熊燃烧哪。

**Hysilens:** From that page...

> **海瑟音：**从那书页中……

**Hysilens:** Emerged... an ocean?

> **海瑟音：**变出了…一片大海？

**Hysilens:** So vast. So... pure.

> **海瑟音：**如此辽阔，如此…纯洁……

**Hysilens:** Not a single trace of corruption...

> **海瑟音：**没有一丝…污浊的气息。

**Hysilens:** Is this...

> **海瑟音：**这是……

**Hysilens:** Cerydra...?

> **海瑟音：**刻律德菈…？

**Cerydra:** "Helektra, my subject..."

> **刻律德菈：**「海列屈拉，我的臣子——」

**Cerydra:** "Ever since you walked out of the tainted ocean and pledged yourself to me, I have seen the abyss in your eyes and the emptiness in your heart."

> **刻律德菈：**「自你走出被污染的海洋，投入我的麾下，我便窥见你眼眸中的深渊，你内心的空洞。」

**Cerydra:** "That was why I promised you a new ocean, where the Imperator's ambitions would serve as the currents and loyalty would be its light."

> **刻律德菈：**「于是，我承诺你一片崭新的海域，将凯撒的野心视作洋流，将忠诚视作火光。」

**Cerydra:** "Helektra, my subject, you have done your duty as my blade. The prophecy has now made its final decree on the road I intend to walk: My conquest is doomed to never reach the stars."

> **刻律德菈：**「海列屈拉，我的臣子——你已尽锋刃之责。如今，神谕为我欲行之路作了断言：我的征服，注定无法迈向群星。」

**Cerydra:** "But the Imperator never makes empty promises. So, take this memory, this ocean beyond the sky, as witnessed by the Deliverer."

> **刻律德菈：**「但，凯撒的承诺从不落空，所以，收下这篇记忆，那救世主之所见，天外的大海吧。」

**Cerydra:** "This is the final gift... that the Imperator can leave for her subject."

> **刻律德菈：**「这是凯撒所能留予一位臣子的——最后的赐赏。」

**Hysilens:** ...

> **海瑟音：**……

**Hysilens:** ...Hah.

> **海瑟音：**…呵。

**Hysilens:** When I was still a fish in the ocean, I always felt so genuine swimming through the waters.

> **海瑟音：**很久以前，当我还是一尾海中的鱼儿，在水中畅游的时分总是那么真实。

**Hysilens:** But once I stepped onto land, I found myself caught in a play. Every land-dweller wore a mask in their life. So I, too, donned a costume and tried to find my role in this show. I became your blade in war and the violin strings at banquets...

> **海瑟音：**但踏上陆地后，我便陷入了一出戏剧。地上的人们都戴着面具生活。于是我也穿上戏服，试图寻找自己的角色。我成为了你的利剑，宴会上的琴弦……

**Hysilens:** Do you still not get it? I do not long for the oceanic depths of my homeland, nor do I crave the swelling seas beyond the sky.

> **海瑟音：**你还不明白吗？我既不眷恋故国的深海，也不向往天外的汪洋。

**Hysilens:** ...

> **海瑟音：**……

**Hysilens:** Perhaps even I don't know what I want. I still don't know where I should swim to, even at the end.

> **海瑟音：**或许，我自己也不明白吧。到最后都不知道自己该游向何处。

**Hysilens:** But I know one thing right now.

> **海瑟音：**但现在，只有一件事，我十分清楚。

**Hysilens:** A fish is simply a fish, and she can never leave the ocean she's in.

> **海瑟音：**鱼儿终究是鱼儿，怎能离开她栖身的海洋？

**Hysilens:** But, whether under the ocean or on land, we two prisoners who can never escape this cave...

> **海瑟音：**但无论深海或大地，我或者你，两位无从逃离洞穴的囚徒……

**Hysilens:** Can still look outside the cave and see the stars.

> **海瑟音：**也能够看向洞外，仰望星光。

### Glimpse the beginning of the next story

**"Zandar":** I have to admit, the twelve factors' emulation of life in this experiment has far exceeded my expectations.

> **「赞达尔」：**不得不承认：在这场实验中，十二因子对生命行为的模拟已经远超预期。

**Herta:** Make your choice then, Senior Society Member. Do you want to keep walking down the wrong path and be remembered for your incorrect conclusions...

> **黑塔：**所以，自己选吧，前辈。是一意孤行，让一句错误的结论成为你的遗言……

**Herta:** Or do you want to return to the audience's seat and give yourself, Amphoreus, and the entire cosmos a more fitting end?

> **黑塔：**还是退回观众席，给自己、翁法罗斯还有整片银河一个更好的交代。

**"Zandar":** I must refuse. A dead man never fears death.
